292	## 执行指引
293	
294	### 执行顺序
295	
296	1. **P0 全部**（4 项，预估 35 分钟）→ build + lint 验证
297	2. **P1 全部**（7 项，预估 105 分钟）→ build + lint 验证
298	3. **P3 顺手改**（15 项，预估 60 分钟）→ build + lint 验证
299	4. **P2 拆分**（按迭代计划排期，不在本对话执行）
300	
301	### P0-P1 每项修复后
302	
303	- `pnpm -C rewrite build`
304	- `pnpm -C rewrite lint`
305	- 如失败，修复后再验证
306	
307	### 全部完成后
308	
309	- 运行 `pnpm -C rewrite test`（如有相关测试）
310	- 在修复文件中 grep 确认：
311	  - `catch {}` / `catch () {}` 无残留
312	  - `deepClone` 只在 shared/ 定义
313	  - `hexToBytes` 只在一处定义
314	  - `formatElapsed` 只在 shared/ 定义
315	  - `noopVariableProvider` / `NOOP_VARIABLE_PROVIDER` 只在一处定义
316	- 产出实施摘要：
317	  - `Changed files:` 改了什么文件、改了什么
318	  - `Verify evidence:` 跑了什么验证命令、结果是什么
319	  - `Open issues:` P2 拆分项和暂未修复的问题
320	
321	### 不做什么
322	
323	- 不执行 P2 拆分（下一迭代）
324	- 不改旧系统代码
325	- 不改 Electron main/preload 层
326	- 不改 frame/connection 页面（已审计过）
327	- 不改测试文件
328	- 不引入新依赖
329

1	export type HighlightSeverity = 'info' | 'warning' | 'error';
2	
3	/**
4	 * Data-driven highlight rule — fully serializable (no functions).
5	 */
6	export interface HighlightRuleConfig {
7	  readonly id: string;
8	  readonly offset: number;
9	  readonly length: number;
10	  readonly pattern?: string;
11	  readonly severity: HighlightSeverity;
12	}
13	
14	/**
15	 * @deprecated Use HighlightRuleConfig instead. The `match` function prevents serialization.
16	 */
17	export interface HighlightRule {
18	  readonly id: string;
19	  match: (value: unknown) => boolean;
20	  readonly severity: HighlightSeverity;
21	}
22	
23	export interface Highlight {
24	  readonly ruleId: string;
25	  readonly severity: HighlightSeverity;
26	  readonly matched: true;
27	}
28	
29	export function calculateHighlights(
30	  data: Uint8Array,
31	  rules: readonly HighlightRuleConfig[],
32	): Highlight[];
33	
34	/** @deprecated Use the Uint8Array + HighlightRuleConfig overload */
35	export function calculateHighlights(
36	  data: Record<string, unknown>,
37	  rules: readonly HighlightRule[],
38	): Highlight[];
39	
40	export function calculateHighlights(
41	  data: Uint8Array | Record<string, unknown>,
42	  rules: readonly (HighlightRuleConfig | HighlightRule)[],
43	): Highlight[] {
44	  const results: Highlight[] = [];
45	
46	  if (data instanceof Uint8Array) {
47	    for (const rule of rules) {
48	      if (!('offset' in rule)) continue;
49	      const cfg = rule as HighlightRuleConfig;
50	      if (cfg.offset + cfg.length > data.length) continue;
51	
52	      if (!cfg.pattern) {
53	        results.push({ ruleId: cfg.id, severity: cfg.severity, matched: true });
54	        continue;
55	      }
56	
57	      const patternBytes = hexToBytes(cfg.pattern);
58	      let match = true;
59	      for (let i = 0; i < patternBytes.length && i < cfg.length; i++) {
60	        if (data[cfg.offset + i] !== patternBytes[i]) {
61	          match = false;
62	          break;
63	        }
64	      }
65	      if (match) {
66	        results.push({ ruleId: cfg.id, severity: cfg.severity, matched: true });
67	      }
68	    }
69	  } else {
70	    for (const rule of rules) {
71	      if (!('match' in rule)) continue;
72	      const legacy = rule as HighlightRule;
73	      for (const value of Object.values(data)) {
74	        if (legacy.match(value)) {
75	          results.push({ ruleId: legacy.id, severity: legacy.severity, matched: true });
76	          break;
77	        }
78	      }
79	    }
80	  }
81	
82	  return results;
83	}
84	
85	function hexToBytes(hex: string): number[] {
86	  const cleaned = hex.replace(/\s/g, '');
87	  const bytes: number[] = [];
88	  for (let i = 0; i < cleaned.length; i += 2) {
89	    bytes.push(parseInt(cleaned.substring(i, i + 2), 16));
90	  }
91	  return bytes;
92	}
93	
```

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { computed } from 'vue';
3	import type { TaskInstanceState, TaskProgress, TaskStepDefinition, TaskStepResult } from '@/features/task';
4	import { STEP_KIND_LABELS } from '@/features/task/components/task-labels';
5	import StatusBadge from './StatusBadge.vue';
6	import { TASK_STATUS_MAP } from '@/features/task/components/taskStatusMap';
7	import { isStepResultFailed } from '@/features/task';
8	
9	const props = defineProps<{
10	  readonly instance: TaskInstanceState;
11	  readonly progress: TaskProgress | null;
12	  readonly displayStatus: string;
13	  readonly statusInfo: { label: string; color: string };
14	}>();
15	
16	const emit = defineEmits<{
17	  pause: [];
18	  resume: [];
19	  stop: [];
20	}>();
21	
22	const stepStatuses = computed(() => {
23	  const steps = props.instance.definitionRef.steps;
24	  const results = props.instance.stepResults;
25	  const currentIter = props.instance.currentIteration;
26	  const currentStepIdx = props.instance.currentStepIndex;
27	
28	  const currentIterResults = results.filter((r) => r.iteration === currentIter);
29	  const resultMap = new Map<number, TaskStepResult>();
30	  for (const r of currentIterResults) {
31	    resultMap.set(r.stepIndex, r);
32	  }
33	
34	  return steps.map((step, idx) => {
35	    const result = resultMap.get(idx);
36	    let status: 'pending' | 'running' | 'done' | 'failed' | 'skipped' = 'pending';
37	    if (result) {
38	      if (result.appliedPolicy === 'skip-step') {
39	        status = 'skipped';
40	      } else if (isStepResultFailed(result)) {
41	        status = 'failed';
42	      } else {
43	        status = 'done';
44	      }
45	    } else if (idx === currentStepIdx) {
46	      status = 'running';
47	    }
48	    return { step, index: idx, status, result };
49	  });
50	});
51	
52	function formatElapsed(ms: number): string {
53	  if (ms < 1000) return `${ms}ms`;
54	  const s = Math.floor(ms / 1000);
55	  if (s < 60) return `${s}s`;
56	  const m = Math.floor(s / 60);
57	  const rs = s % 60;
58	  if (m < 60) return `${m}m ${rs}s`;
59	  const h = Math.floor(m / 60);
60	  const rm = m % 60;
61	  return `${h}h ${rm}m`;
62	}
63	
64	function formatStepResult(step: TaskStepDefinition, result: TaskStepResult | undefined): string {
65	  if (!result) return '';
66	  switch (step.kind) {
67	    case 'send':
68	      return result.kind === 'send'
69	        ? result.sendResult.kind === 'sent' ? '发送成功' : '发送失败'
70	        : '';
71	    case 'wait-condition':
72	      return result.kind === 'wait-condition'
73	        ? result.matched ? '条件匹配' : result.timedOut ? '超时' : ''
74	        : '';
75	    case 'delay':
76	      return result.kind === 'delay'
77	        ? result.completed ? '完成' : '中断'
78	        : '';
79	  }
80	}
81	
82	const isRunning = computed(() => props.instance.lifecycle === 'running');
83	const isPaused = computed(() => props.instance.lifecycle === 'paused');
84	
85	const progressPct = computed(() => {
86	  if (!props.progress) return 0;
87	  const total = props.progress.stepsTotal || 1;
88	  return Math.round((props.progress.stepsCompleted / total) * 100);
89	});
90	</script>
91	
92	<template>
93	  <div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
94	    <!-- Header -->
95	    <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
96	      <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
97	        <StatusBadge :status="displayStatus" :status-map="TASK_STATUS_MAP" />
98	        <span class="rw-text-value text-sm">{{ instance.definitionRef.name }}</span>
99	      </div>
100	    </div>
101	
102	    <!-- Progress -->
103	    <div v-if="progress" class="flex flex-col gap-1">
104	      <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
105	        <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">进度</span>
106	        <span class="rw-text-value text-xs">{{ progress.stepsCompleted }}/{{ progress.stepsTotal }} ({{ progressPct }}%)</span>
107	      </div>
108	      <q-linear-progress :value="progressPct / 100" color="primary" size="6px" rounded />
109	    </div>
110	
111	    <!-- Elapsed -->
112	    <div v-if="progress" class="flex items-center justify-between">
113	      <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">耗时</span>
114	      <span class="rw-text-value text-xs">{{ formatElapsed(progress.elapsedMs) }}</span>
115	    </div>
116	
117	    <!-- Iteration info -->
118	    <div v-if="progress && progress.iterationsTotal !== null" class="flex items-center justify-between">
119	      <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">迭代</span>
120	      <span class="rw-text-value text-xs">{{ progress.iterationsCompleted }}/{{ progress.iterationsTotal }}</span>
121	    </div>
122	
123	    <!-- Step status list -->
124	    <q-separator />
125	    <div class="flex flex-col gap-1">
126	      <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">步骤状态</span>
127	      <div
128	        v-for="item in stepStatuses"
129	        :key="item.index"
130	        class="flex items-center gap-2"
131	      >
132	        <q-icon
133	          :name="item.status === 'running' ? 'o_pending' : item.status === 'done' ? 'o_check_circle' : item.status === 'failed' ? 'o_error' : item.status === 'skipped' ? 'o_skip_next' : 'o_radio_button_unchecked'"
134	          :color="item.status === 'done' ? 'positive' : item.status === 'failed' ? 'negative' : item.status === 'running' ? 'primary' : 'grey'"
135	          size="xs"
136	        />
137	        <span class="rw-text-value text-xs flex-1">
138	          {{ item.step.name ?? STEP_KIND_LABELS[item.step.kind].label }} #{{ item.index + 1 }}
139	        </span>
140	        <span v-if="item.result" class="rw-text-desc text-xs">
141	          {{ formatStepResult(item.step, item.result) }}
142	        </span>
143	      </div>
144	    </div>
145	
146	    <!-- Error -->
147	    <div v-if="instance.error" class="rw-text-error text-xs p-2 rounded" style="background: var(--rw-color-surface-elevated)">
148	      {{ instance.error }}
149	    </div>
150	
151	    <!-- Actions -->
152	    <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
153	      <q-btn
154	        v-if="isRunning"
155	        flat
156	        no-caps
157	        dense
158	        icon="o_pause"
159	        label="暂停"
160	        color="warning"
161	        size="sm"
162	        @click="emit('pause')"
163	      />
164	      <q-btn
165	        v-if="isPaused"
166	        flat
167	        no-caps
168	        dense
169	        icon="o_play_arrow"
170	        label="恢复"
171	        color="primary"
172	        size="sm"
173	        @click="emit('resume')"
174	      />
175	      <q-btn
176	        v-if="isRunning || isPaused"
177	        flat
178	        no-caps
179	        dense
180	        icon="o_stop"
181	        label="停止"
182	        color="negative"
183	        size="sm"
184	        @click="emit('stop')"
185	      />
186	    </div>
187	  </div>
188	</template>
189

1	import type { TaskService } from '@/features/task';
2	import type { SendService } from '@/features/send';
3	import type { ConnectionService, ConnectionStateSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';
4	import type { FrameAssetReader } from '@/features/frame';
5	import type { ProtocolAdapter } from '../core/protocol-adapter';
6	import type { CommandContext, CommandHandler, CommandResult } from '../core/handler';
7	import type { ScoeCommandFunction, SatelliteConfig, ScoeStatisticsSnapshot, ScoeRuntimeStatus } from '../core/types';
8	import type { CommandIngressStateReader, CommandIngressStateWriter } from '../core/state';
9	import type { ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions } from '../adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter';
10	import type { CommandLogEntry } from '../core/command-log';
11	import type { DataRecorder } from './data-recorder';
12	import { dispatchCommand } from '../core/handler';
13	import { createScoeProtocolAdapter } from '../adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter';
14	import { createHandleLoadSatelliteId } from '../handlers/handle-load';
15	import { handleUnloadSatelliteId } from '../handlers/handle-unload';
16	import { handleHealthCheck } from '../handlers/handle-health-check';
17	import { handleLinkCheck } from '../handlers/handle-link-check';
18	import { handleSendFrame } from '../handlers/handle-send-frame';
19	import { handleReadFileAndSend } from '../handlers/handle-read-file-and-send';
20	import { sendAckFrame } from '../handlers/send-ack-frame';
21	import { createCommandLogRecorder } from '../core/command-log';
22	import { createDataRecorder } from './data-recorder';
23	
24	export interface CommandIngressServiceOptions {
25	  readonly globalConfig: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions['globalConfig'];
26	  readonly commandConfigs: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions['commandConfigs'];
27	  readonly satelliteConfigs: readonly SatelliteConfig[];
28	  readonly taskService: TaskService;
29	  readonly sendService: SendService;
30	  readonly frameReader: FrameAssetReader;
31	  readonly connectionService: ConnectionService;
32	  readonly connectionSnapshot: () => ConnectionStateSnapshot;
33	  readonly receiveSnapshot: () => unknown;
34	  readonly platformFileReader: (path: string) => Promise<string[]>;
35	  readonly stateReader: CommandIngressStateReader;
36	  readonly stateWriter: CommandIngressStateWriter;
37	}
38	
39	export interface CommandIngressService {
40	  readonly adapter: ProtocolAdapter;
41	  loadSatellite(satelliteId: string): Promise<CommandResult>;
42	  unloadSatellite(): Promise<CommandResult>;
43	  getScoeStatistics(): ScoeStatisticsSnapshot;
44	  getScoeRuntimeStatus(): ScoeRuntimeStatus;
45	  getLoadedSatelliteId(): string;
46	  isScoeFramesLoaded(): boolean;
47	  getCommandLog(): readonly CommandLogEntry[];
48	  clearCommandLog(): void;
49	  getTestDataRecorder(): DataRecorder;
50	  sendTestData(hex: string, connectionId: string): Promise<void>;
51	  dispose(): void;
52	}
53	
54	export function createCommandIngressService(
55	  options: CommandIngressServiceOptions,
56	): CommandIngressService {
57	  const {
58	    satelliteConfigs,
59	    taskService,
60	    sendService,
61	    frameReader,
62	    connectionService,
63	    connectionSnapshot,
64	    receiveSnapshot,
65	    platformFileReader,
66	    stateReader,
67	    stateWriter,
68	  } = options;
69	
70	  // Tracking: connectionId → taskInstanceIds
71	  const trackingMap = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
72	
73	  // C1: Command log recorder
74	  const logRecorder = createCommandLogRecorder();
75	
76	  // C2: Test data recorder
77	  const testDataRecorder = createDataRecorder();
78	
79	  // Handler map
80	  const handlerMap = new Map<ScoeCommandFunction, CommandHandler>([
81	    ['load_satellite_id', createHandleLoadSatelliteId(satelliteConfigs)],
82	    ['unload_satellite_id', handleUnloadSatelliteId],
83	    ['health_check', handleHealthCheck],
84	    ['link_check', handleLinkCheck],
85	    ['send_frame', handleSendFrame],
86	    ['read_file_and_send', handleReadFileAndSend],
87	  ]);
88	
89	  const ctx: CommandContext = {
90	    taskService,
91	    sendService,
92	    frameReader,
93	    connectionService,
94	    connectionSnapshot,
95	    receiveSnapshot,
96	    platformFileReader,
97	    stateReader,
98	    stateWriter,
99	  };
100	
101	  // onCommand: dispatch parsed commands, track tasks, monitor settlement
102	  async function onCommand(parsed: Parameters<typeof dispatchCommand>[0]): Promise<void> {
103	    stateWriter.updateStatus({ lastCommandCode: parsed.commandCode });
104	
105	    const logEntry = logRecorder.record({
106	      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
107	      commandCode: parsed.commandCode,
108	      result: 'pending',
109	    });
110	
111	    const result = await dispatchCommand(parsed, handlerMap, ctx);
112	
113	    if (result.taskId) {
114	      trackTask(parsed.connectionId, result.taskId, parsed, logEntry.id);
115	    } else {
116	      logRecorder.complete(logEntry.id, {
117	        result: result.success ? 'success' : 'error',
118	        error: result.error,
119	      });
120	    }
121	
122	    cleanupDisconnectedConnections();
123	  }
124	
125	  // Create protocol adapter with onCommand dispatch
126	  const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({
127	    globalConfig: options.globalConfig,
128	    commandConfigs: options.commandConfigs,
129	    stateReader,
130	    onCommand,
131	    onConsume: (bytes) => testDataRecorder.record(bytes),
132	  });
133	
134	  // Start status timer (inlined from status-timer.ts)
135	  const timerHandle = setInterval(() => {
136	    stateWriter.tickSecond();
137	  }, 1000);
138	
139	  function trackTask(
140	    connectionId: string,
141	    taskId: string,
142	    command: Parameters<typeof dispatchCommand>[0],
143	    logEntryId: string,
144	  ): void {
145	    let set = trackingMap.get(connectionId);
146	    if (!set) {
147	      set = new Set();
148	      trackingMap.set(connectionId, set);
149	    }
150	    set.add(taskId);
151	
152	    // Single consolidated settlement monitor: cleanup tracking + ack frame + counters
153	    taskService.onSettled(taskId)
154	      .then(async () => {
155	        const inst = taskService.getInstance(taskId);
156	        if (inst?.lifecycle === 'completed') {
157	          try {
158	            await sendAckFrame(command, ctx);
159	          } catch {
160	            // Ack frame send failure — don't crash, just count as error
161	          }
162	          stateWriter.incrementSuccessCount();
163	          logRecorder.complete(logEntryId, {
164	            result: 'success',
165	            durationMs: inst.completedAt && inst.startedAt
166	              ? new Date(inst.completedAt).getTime() - new Date(inst.startedAt).getTime()
167	              : undefined,
168	          });
169	        } else {
170	          stateWriter.incrementErrorCount(inst?.error ?? 'task failed');
171	          logRecorder.complete(logEntryId, {
172	            result: 'error',
173	            error: inst?.error ?? 'task failed',
174	          });
175	        }
176	
177	        // Re-lookup Set from trackingMap (avoids stale closure reference)
178	        const current = trackingMap.get(connectionId);
179	        if (!current) return;
180	        current.delete(taskId);
181	        if (current.size === 0) {
182	          trackingMap.delete(connectionId);
183	        }
184	      })
185	      .catch((err) => {
186	        stateWriter.incrementErrorCount(
187	          err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'settlement tracking failed',
188	        );
189	        // Still cleanup tracking
190	        const current = trackingMap.get(connectionId);
191	        if (current) {
192	          current.delete(taskId);
193	          if (current.size === 0) trackingMap.delete(connectionId);
194	        }
195	      });
196	  }
197	
198	  function cleanupDisconnectedConnections(): void {
199	    for (const [connId, taskIds] of trackingMap) {
200	      const fact = connectionService.getConnectionFact(connId);
201	      if (!fact || fact.lifecycle === 'disconnected') {
202	        for (const taskId of taskIds) {
203	          try {
204	            taskService.stopTask(taskId);
205	          } catch {
206	            // Task may already be settled
207	          }
208	        }
209	        trackingMap.delete(connId);
210	      }
211	    }
212	  }
213	
214	  function dispose(): void {
215	    clearInterval(timerHandle);
216	    for (const [, taskIds] of trackingMap) {
217	      for (const taskId of taskIds) {
218	        try {
219	          taskService.stopTask(taskId);
220	        } catch {
221	          // Ignore errors during cleanup
222	        }
223	      }
224	    }
225	    trackingMap.clear();
226	  }
227	
228	  // CI-R2: delegate loadSatellite to handler logic
229	  createHandleLoadSatelliteId(satelliteConfigs);
230	  async function loadSatellite(satelliteId: string): Promise<CommandResult> {
231	    if (stateReader.loadedSatelliteId !== '') {
232	      return { success: false, error: 'Satellite already loaded' };
233	    }
234	    const satConfig = satelliteConfigs.find((s) => s.satelliteId === satelliteId);
235	    if (!satConfig) {
236	      return { success: false, error: `Unknown satellite ID: ${satelliteId}` };
237	    }
238	    stateWriter.setLoaded(satelliteId, satConfig.commandConfigs);
239	    return { success: true };
240	  }
241	
242	  async function unloadSatellite(): Promise<CommandResult> {
243	    if (!stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded) {
244	      return { success: false, error: 'No satellite loaded' };
245	    }
246	    stateWriter.resetRuntimeState();
247	    return { success: true };
248	  }
249	
250	  // CI-R5: Reader methods replacing deleted selectors
251	  function getScoeStatistics(): ScoeStatisticsSnapshot {
252	    const s = stateReader.getSnapshot();
253	    return {
254	      commandReceiveCount: s.commandReceiveCount,
255	      commandSuccessCount: s.commandSuccessCount,
256	      commandErrorCount: s.commandErrorCount,
257	      runtimeSeconds: s.runtimeSeconds,
258	      satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: s.satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds,
259	      lastErrorReason: s.lastErrorReason,
260	    };
261	  }
262	
263	  function getScoeRuntimeStatus(): ScoeRuntimeStatus {
264	    const s = stateReader.getSnapshot();
265	    return {
266	      loadedSatelliteId: s.loadedSatelliteId,
267	      scoeFramesLoaded: s.scoeFramesLoaded,
268	      healthStatus: s.healthStatus,
269	      linkTestResult: s.linkTestResult,
270	      lastCommandCode: s.lastCommandCode,
271	      receiveCommandSuccess: s.receiveCommandSuccess,
272	    };
273	  }
274	
275	  function getLoadedSatelliteId(): string {
276	    return stateReader.loadedSatelliteId;
277	  }
278	
279	  function isScoeFramesLoaded(): boolean {
280	    return stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded;
281	  }
282	
283	  function getCommandLog(): readonly CommandLogEntry[] {
284	    return logRecorder.getAll();
285	  }
286	
287	  function clearCommandLog(): void {
288	    logRecorder.clear();
289	  }
290	
291	  function getTestDataRecorder(): DataRecorder {
292	    return testDataRecorder;
293	  }
294	
295	  async function sendTestData(hex: string, connectionId: string): Promise<void> {
296	    const bytes = hexToBytes(hex);
297	    await connectionService.write({ connectionId, bytes });
298	  }
299	
300	  return {
301	    adapter,
302	    loadSatellite,
303	    unloadSatellite,
304	    getScoeStatistics,
305	    getScoeRuntimeStatus,
306	    getLoadedSatelliteId,
307	    isScoeFramesLoaded,
308	    getCommandLog,
309	    clearCommandLog,
310	    getTestDataRecorder,
311	    sendTestData,
312	    dispose,
313	  };
314	}
315	
316	function hexToBytes(hex: string): number[] {
317	  const cleaned = hex.replace(/\s/g, '');
318	  if (cleaned.length % 2 !== 0) {
319	    throw new Error('Hex string must have even length');
320	  }
321	  const bytes: number[] = [];
322	  for (let i = 0; i < cleaned.length; i += 2) {
323	    bytes.push(parseInt(cleaned.substring(i, i + 2), 16));
324	  }
325	  return bytes;
326	}
327

1	import type {
2	  ReadonlySendStateSnapshot,
3	  SendResult,
4	  SendServiceStatus,
5	  SendStateSnapshot,
6	  SendStatisticsSnapshot,
7	  SendStatsDelta,
8	} from '../core';
9	
10	const MAX_RECENT_RESULTS = 100;
11	
12	function deepClone<T>(value: T): T { return structuredClone(value); }
13	
14	function emptyStatistics(): SendStatisticsSnapshot {
15	  return {
16	    totalRequests: 0,
17	    totalSent: 0,
18	    totalErrors: 0,
19	    totalBytesSent: 0,
20	    byFrame: {},
21	    byTarget: {},
22	    byResultKind: {},
23	  };
24	}
25	
26	function emptySnapshot(): SendStateSnapshot {
27	  return {
28	    status: 'idle',
29	    statistics: emptyStatistics(),
30	    recentResults: [],
31	  };
32	}
33	
34	function applyStatsDelta(stats: SendStatisticsSnapshot, delta: SendStatsDelta): SendStatisticsSnapshot {
35	  const isSent = delta.resultKind === 'sent';
36	  const byFrame = { ...stats.byFrame };
37	  const prevFrame = byFrame[delta.frameId] ?? { sent: 0, errors: 0 };
38	  byFrame[delta.frameId] = {
39	    sent: prevFrame.sent + (isSent ? 1 : 0),
40	    errors: prevFrame.errors + (isSent ? 0 : 1),
41	  };
42	
43	  const byTarget = { ...stats.byTarget };
44	  const prevTarget = byTarget[delta.targetId] ?? { sent: 0, errors: 0 };
45	  byTarget[delta.targetId] = {
46	    sent: prevTarget.sent + (isSent ? 1 : 0),
47	    errors: prevTarget.errors + (isSent ? 0 : 1),
48	  };
49	
50	  const byResultKind = { ...stats.byResultKind };
51	  byResultKind[delta.resultKind] = (byResultKind[delta.resultKind] ?? 0) + 1;
52	
53	  return {
54	    totalRequests: stats.totalRequests + 1,
55	    totalSent: stats.totalSent + (isSent ? 1 : 0),
56	    totalErrors: stats.totalErrors + (isSent ? 0 : 1),
57	    totalBytesSent: stats.totalBytesSent + delta.bytesSent,
58	    byFrame,
59	    byTarget,
60	    byResultKind,
61	    lastSendAt: delta.timestamp,
62	  };
63	}
64	
65	export interface SendStateContainer {
66	  getSnapshot(): SendStateSnapshot;
67	  setStatus(status: SendServiceStatus): SendStateSnapshot;
68	  addResult(result: SendResult): SendStateSnapshot;
69	  resetStats(): SendStateSnapshot;
70	  resetSnapshot(snapshot?: ReadonlySendStateSnapshot): SendStateSnapshot;
71	}
72	
73	export function createSendState(
74	  initialValue: { readonly snapshot?: ReadonlySendStateSnapshot } = {},
75	): SendStateContainer {
76	  let snapshot = initialValue.snapshot
77	    ? deepClone(initialValue.snapshot)
78	    : emptySnapshot();
79	
80	  return {
81	    getSnapshot() {
82	      return deepClone(snapshot);
83	    },
84	
85	    setStatus(status) {
86	      snapshot = { ...deepClone(snapshot), status };
87	      return deepClone(snapshot);
88	    },
89	
90	    addResult(result) {
91	      const cloned = deepClone(snapshot);
92	      const delta: SendStatsDelta = {
93	        frameId: result.requestRef.frameId,
94	        targetId: result.requestRef.targetId,
95	        resultKind: result.kind,
96	        bytesSent: result.bytesSent,
97	        timestamp: result.timestamp,
98	      };
99	      const recentResults = [...cloned.recentResults, result].slice(-MAX_RECENT_RESULTS);
100	      snapshot = {
101	        ...cloned,
102	        statistics: applyStatsDelta(cloned.statistics, delta),
103	        recentResults,
104	        status: result.kind === 'sent' ? 'idle' : 'error',
105	        ...(result.error ? { lastError: result.error } : {}),
106	      };
107	      return deepClone(snapshot);
108	    },
109	
110	    resetStats() {
111	      snapshot = { ...deepClone(snapshot), statistics: emptyStatistics() };
112	      return deepClone(snapshot);
113	    },
114	
115	    resetSnapshot(next = emptySnapshot()) {
116	      snapshot = deepClone(next);
117	      return deepClone(snapshot);
118	    },
119	  };
120	}
121	
```

1	import { computed, type Ref } from 'vue';
2	import type { ReadonlyFrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';
3	import type { SendFieldValue, SendVariableProvider } from '../core';
4	import {
5	  frameToBuildInput,
6	  resolveFieldValues,
7	  applyFactor,
8	  buildFrame,
9	  applyBuildPostPatch,
10	  evaluateFieldPreviewForUI,
11	} from '../core';
12	
13	// GS2: noop variable provider — used when runtime variableProvider is not exposed to UI
14	const noopVariableProvider: SendVariableProvider = {
15	  getVariables: () => new Map(),
16	};
17	
18	export interface FramePreviewResult {
19	  readonly hexPreview: string;
20	  readonly fieldPreviews: ReadonlyArray<{
21	    readonly fieldId: string;
22	    readonly fieldName: string;
23	    readonly value: SendFieldValue;
24	  }>;
25	  readonly issues: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly severity: string; readonly message: string }>;
26	}
27	
28	export function useFramePreview(
29	  frame: Ref<ReadonlyFrameAsset | null>,
30	  userFieldValues: Ref<Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>>,
31	) {
32	  const fullPreview = computed<FramePreviewResult>(() => {
33	    const f = frame.value;
34	    if (!f) {
35	      return { hexPreview: '', fieldPreviews: [], issues: [] };
36	    }
37	
38	    try {
39	      const { fields, totalByteLength } = frameToBuildInput(f);
40	      const provider = noopVariableProvider;
41	
42	      // Resolve all field values
43	      const resolved = resolveFieldValues(fields, userFieldValues.value, provider);
44	      const factored = applyFactor(fields, resolved.values);
45	      const allIssues = [...resolved.issues, ...factored.issues];
46	
47	      // Build frame bytes
48	      const buildOutput = buildFrame({
49	        fields,
50	        totalByteLength,
51	        fieldValues: factored.values,
52	      });
53	      allIssues.push(...buildOutput.issues);
54	
55	      // Apply post-patch (checksum, length)
56	      const patched = applyBuildPostPatch(buildOutput.bytes, fields, {
57	        autoChecksum: f.options?.autoChecksum,
58	        bigEndian: f.options?.bigEndian,
59	        includeLengthField: f.options?.includeLengthField,
60	      });
61	      allIssues.push(...patched.issues);
62	
63	      // Format hex preview: byte-space-separated
64	      const hexBytes = [...patched.buffer]
65	        .map((b) => b.toString(16).toUpperCase().padStart(2, '0'));
66	      const hexPreview = hexBytes.join(' ');
67	
68	      // Per-field previews
69	      const fieldPreviews = f.fields.map((field) => {
70	        const preview = evaluateFieldPreviewForUI(
71	          f,
72	          field.id,
73	          userFieldValues.value,
74	          provider,
75	        );
76	        return {
77	          fieldId: field.id,
78	          fieldName: field.name,
79	          value: preview.value,
80	        };
81	      });
82	
83	      return {
84	        hexPreview,
85	        fieldPreviews,
86	        issues: allIssues.map((i) => ({ severity: i.severity, message: i.message })),
87	      };
88	    } catch {
89	      return { hexPreview: '', fieldPreviews: [], issues: [] };
90	    }
91	  });
92	
93	  return { fullPreview };
94	}
95	
```

1	import type { ReadonlyDeep } from '@/shared/types/readonly-deep';
2	import type {
3	  TaskDefinition,
4	  TaskInstanceState,
5	  TaskStepResult,
6	} from '../core';
7	import { isStepResultFailed } from '../core';
8	
9	const MAX_HISTORY = 50;
10	const DEFAULT_MAX_STEP_RESULT_ITERATIONS = 100;
11	
12	// --- Helpers ---
13	
14	function deepClone<T>(value: T): T {
15	  return structuredClone(value);
16	}
17	
18	// --- Statistics ---
19	
20	export interface TaskStatisticsSnapshot {
21	  readonly totalCreated: number;
22	  readonly totalCompleted: number;
23	  readonly totalStopped: number;
24	  readonly totalFailed: number;
25	  readonly totalStepsExecuted: number;
26	  readonly totalStepsSucceeded: number;
27	  readonly totalStepsFailed: number;
28	  readonly totalStepsSkipped: number;
29	}
30	
31	// --- State snapshot ---
32	
33	export interface TaskStateSnapshot {
34	  readonly instances: readonly TaskInstanceState[];
35	  readonly history: readonly TaskInstanceState[];
36	  readonly statistics: TaskStatisticsSnapshot;
37	}
38	
39	export type ReadonlyTaskStateSnapshot = ReadonlyDeep<TaskStateSnapshot>;
40	
41	// --- Container ---
42	
43	export interface TaskStateContainer {
44	  getSnapshot(): TaskStateSnapshot;
45	  createInstance(instanceId: string, definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState;
46	  updateInstance(
47	    instanceId: string,
48	    updates: Partial<Omit<TaskInstanceState, 'instanceId'>>,
49	  ): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
50	  removeInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
51	  addStepResult(instanceId: string, result: TaskStepResult): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
52	  getInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
53	  moveToHistory(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
54	  resetStats(): TaskStateSnapshot;
55	}
56	
57	function emptyStatistics(): TaskStatisticsSnapshot {
58	  return {
59	    totalCreated: 0,
60	    totalCompleted: 0,
61	    totalStopped: 0,
62	    totalFailed: 0,
63	    totalStepsExecuted: 0,
64	    totalStepsSucceeded: 0,
65	    totalStepsFailed: 0,
66	    totalStepsSkipped: 0,
67	  };
68	}
69	
70	// --- Factory ---
71	
72	export function createTaskState(
73	  initialValue?: { readonly snapshot?: ReadonlyTaskStateSnapshot },
74	): TaskStateContainer {
75	  const instances = new Map<string, TaskInstanceState>();
76	  let history: TaskInstanceState[] = [];
77	  let statistics = emptyStatistics();
78	
79	  if (initialValue?.snapshot) {
80	    for (const inst of initialValue.snapshot.instances) {
81	      instances.set(inst.instanceId, deepClone(inst));
82	    }
83	    history = initialValue.snapshot.history.map(deepClone);
84	    statistics = { ...initialValue.snapshot.statistics };
85	  }
86	
87	  function getSnapshot(): TaskStateSnapshot {
88	    return {
89	      instances: [...instances.values()].map(deepClone),
90	      history: history.map(deepClone),
91	      statistics: { ...statistics },
92	    };
93	  }
94	
95	  return {
96	    getSnapshot,
97	
98	    createInstance(instanceId, definition) {
99	      const instance: TaskInstanceState = {
100	        instanceId,
101	        definitionRef: definition,
102	        lifecycle: 'created',
103	        currentStepIndex: 0,
104	        currentIteration: 0,
105	        stepResults: [],
106	      };
107	      instances.set(instanceId, instance);
108	      statistics = { ...statistics, totalCreated: statistics.totalCreated + 1 };
109	      return deepClone(instance);
110	    },
111	
112	    updateInstance(instanceId, updates) {
113	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
114	      if (!existing) return undefined;
115	      const updated: TaskInstanceState = {
116	        ...existing,
117	        ...updates,
118	        instanceId: existing.instanceId,
119	      };
120	      instances.set(instanceId, updated);
121	
122	      if (updates.lifecycle === 'completed') {
123	        statistics = { ...statistics, totalCompleted: statistics.totalCompleted + 1 };
124	      } else if (updates.lifecycle === 'stopped') {
125	        statistics = { ...statistics, totalStopped: statistics.totalStopped + 1 };
126	      } else if (updates.lifecycle === 'failed') {
127	        statistics = { ...statistics, totalFailed: statistics.totalFailed + 1 };
128	      }
129	
130	      return deepClone(updated);
131	    },
132	
133	    removeInstance(instanceId) {
134	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
135	      if (!existing) return undefined;
136	      instances.delete(instanceId);
137	      return deepClone(existing);
138	    },
139	
140	    addStepResult(instanceId, result) {
141	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
142	      if (!existing) return undefined;
143	
144	      const newResults = [...existing.stepResults, deepClone(result)];
145	      const maxIter = Math.max(existing.currentIteration, result.iteration);
146	      const minIteration = Math.max(0, maxIter - DEFAULT_MAX_STEP_RESULT_ITERATIONS + 1);
147	      const boundedResults = newResults.filter((r) => r.iteration >= minIteration);
148	
149	      const updated: TaskInstanceState = {
150	        ...existing,
151	        stepResults: boundedResults,
152	        currentStepIndex: result.stepIndex,
153	        currentIteration: Math.max(existing.currentIteration, result.iteration),
154	      };
155	      instances.set(instanceId, updated);
156	
157	      const isSkipped = result.appliedPolicy === 'skip-step';
158	      const isFailed = !isSkipped && isStepResultFailed(result);
159	      statistics = {
160	        ...statistics,
161	        totalStepsExecuted: statistics.totalStepsExecuted + 1,
162	        totalStepsSucceeded: statistics.totalStepsSucceeded + (isSkipped || isFailed ? 0 : 1),
163	        totalStepsFailed: statistics.totalStepsFailed + (isFailed ? 1 : 0),
164	        totalStepsSkipped: statistics.totalStepsSkipped + (isSkipped ? 1 : 0),
165	      };
166	
167	      return deepClone(updated);
168	    },
169	
170	    getInstance(instanceId) {
171	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
172	      return existing ? deepClone(existing) : undefined;
173	    },
174	
175	    moveToHistory(instanceId) {
176	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
177	      if (!existing) return undefined;
178	      instances.delete(instanceId);
179	      history = [...history, deepClone(existing)].slice(-MAX_HISTORY);
180	      return deepClone(existing);
181	    },
182	
183	    resetStats() {
184	      statistics = emptyStatistics();
185	      return getSnapshot();
186	    },
187	  };
188	}
189	
```

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { ref, shallowRef, computed, onUnmounted } from 'vue';
3	import { useQuasar } from 'quasar';
4	import { useRewriteRuntime } from '@/app/rewriteRuntime';
5	import DataTable from '@/widgets/DataTable.vue';
6	import TableToolbar from '@/widgets/TableToolbar.vue';
7	import StatusBadge from '@/widgets/StatusBadge.vue';
8	import TaskExecutionDetail from '@/widgets/TaskExecutionDetail.vue';
9	import FieldEditWidget from '@/widgets/FieldEditWidget.vue';
10	import SendTargetSelector from '@/features/send/components/SendTargetSelector.vue';
11	import { useAsyncAction, useNotify, usePolling } from '@/shared/composables';
12	import { isTerminal } from '@/features/task/core';
13	import { createSendStep, createDelayStep, createWaitConditionStep } from '@/features/task/core';
14	import type { TaskStepDefinition, SendStepConfig, WaitConditionConfig, ConditionTerm } from '@/features/task/core';
15	import type { ReadonlyTaskInstanceState } from '@/features/task';
16	import { taskColumns, type TaskTableRow } from '@/features/task/components/task-columns';
17	import { historyColumns, type HistoryTableRow } from '@/features/task/components/history-columns';
18	import { TASK_STATUS_MAP, resolveDisplayStatus } from '@/features/task/components/taskStatusMap';
19	import { SCHEDULE_KIND_MAP, SCHEDULE_KIND_OPTIONS } from '@/features/task/components/scheduleKindMap';
20	import { STEP_KIND_LABELS, ERROR_ACTION_LABELS, ON_TIMEOUT_OPTIONS, ADD_STEP_OPTIONS } from '@/features/task/components/task-labels';
21	import { useTaskEditor } from '@/features/task/composables/use-task-editor';
22	
23	const $q = useQuasar();
24	const notify = useNotify();
25	const { execute: executeAction, isOperating } = useAsyncAction();
26	const runtime = useRewriteRuntime();
27	const taskService = runtime.features.taskService;
28	const frameService = runtime.features.frameService;
29	const connectionService = runtime.features.connectionService;
30	
31	const editor = useTaskEditor(taskService);
32	
33	// ===== Tab & filter state =====
34	const activeTab = ref<'active' | 'history'>('active');
35	const searchText = ref('');
36	
37	// ===== Task list data =====
38	const activeRows = shallowRef<TaskTableRow[]>([]);
39	const historyRows = shallowRef<HistoryTableRow[]>([]);
40	const selectedActiveRow = shallowRef<TaskTableRow[]>([]);
41	const selectedHistoryRow = shallowRef<HistoryTableRow[]>([]);
42	
43	const listPolling = usePolling(refreshLists, 1000);
44	listPolling.start();
45	
46	function refreshLists(): void {
47	  const snapshot = taskService.getSnapshot();
48	  const active = snapshot.instances.filter(
49	    (i) => !isTerminal(i.lifecycle),
50	  );
51	  activeRows.value = active.map(toActiveRow);
52	
53	  const terminated = snapshot.instances.filter((i) => isTerminal(i.lifecycle));
54	  const historyIds = new Set(snapshot.history.map((i) => i.instanceId));
55	  const merged = [...snapshot.history, ...terminated.filter((i) => !historyIds.has(i.instanceId))];
56	  historyRows.value = merged.map(toHistoryRow);
57	}
58	
59	function toActiveRow(inst: ReadonlyTaskInstanceState): TaskTableRow {
60	  const displayStatus = resolveDisplayStatus(inst.lifecycle, inst.definitionRef);
61	  const progress = inst.startedAt
62	    ? (() => {
63	        const stepsPerIteration = inst.definitionRef.steps.length;
64	        const currentIterResults = inst.stepResults.filter((r) => r.iteration === inst.currentIteration);
65	        let stepsCompleted = 0;
66	        for (const r of currentIterResults) {
67	          if (r.appliedPolicy !== 'skip-step') stepsCompleted++;
68	        }
69	        return { completed: stepsCompleted, total: stepsPerIteration };
70	      })()
71	    : { completed: 0, total: inst.definitionRef.steps.length };
72	  const pct = progress.total > 0 ? Math.round((progress.completed / progress.total) * 100) : 0;
73	  return {
74	    instanceId: inst.instanceId,
75	    name: inst.definitionRef.name,
76	    scheduleKind: inst.definitionRef.schedule.kind,
77	    lifecycle: inst.lifecycle,
78	    displayStatus,
79	    progressPercent: pct,
80	    progressLabel: `${progress.completed}/${progress.total}`,
81	    _original: inst,
82	  };
83	}
84	
85	function toHistoryRow(inst: ReadonlyTaskInstanceState): HistoryTableRow {
86	  const startedAt = inst.startedAt ? new Date(inst.startedAt).getTime() : 0;
87	  const endRef = inst.completedAt ?? inst.stoppedAt ?? inst.failedAt;
88	  const endTime = endRef ? new Date(endRef).getTime() : Date.now();
89	  const elapsedMs = startedAt > 0 ? Math.max(0, endTime - startedAt) : 0;
90	  return {
91	    instanceId: inst.instanceId,
92	    name: inst.definitionRef.name,
93	    scheduleKind: inst.definitionRef.schedule.kind,
94	    lifecycle: inst.lifecycle,
95	    elapsedMs,
96	    finishedAt: inst.completedAt ?? inst.stoppedAt ?? inst.failedAt ?? '',
97	    _original: inst,
98	  };
99	}
100	
101	onUnmounted(() => {
102	  listPolling.stop();
103	});
104	
105	// ===== Selected instance for right panel =====
106	const selectedInstance = computed<ReadonlyTaskInstanceState | null>(() => {
107	  if (activeTab.value === 'active' && selectedActiveRow.value.length > 0) {
108	    return selectedActiveRow.value[0]._original;
109	  }
110	  if (activeTab.value === 'history' && selectedHistoryRow.value.length > 0) {
111	    return selectedHistoryRow.value[0]._original;
112	  }
113	  return null;
114	});
115	
116	const selectedProgress = computed(() => {
117	  if (!selectedInstance.value) return null;
118	  const inst = selectedInstance.value;
119	  const stepsPerIteration = inst.definitionRef.steps.length;
120	  const currentIterResults = inst.stepResults.filter((r) => r.iteration === inst.currentIteration);
121	  let stepsCompleted = 0;
122	  for (const r of currentIterResults) {
123	    if (r.appliedPolicy !== 'skip-step') stepsCompleted++;
124	  }
125	  return { completed: stepsCompleted, total: stepsPerIteration };
126	});
127	
128	const selectedDisplayStatus = computed(() => {
129	  if (!selectedInstance.value) return '';
130	  return resolveDisplayStatus(selectedInstance.value.lifecycle, selectedInstance.value.definitionRef);
131	});
132	
133	const selectedStatusInfo = computed(() => {
134	  return TASK_STATUS_MAP[selectedDisplayStatus.value] ?? { label: selectedDisplayStatus.value, color: 'grey' };
135	});
136	
137	// ===== Active tab actions =====
138	function onActiveRowClick(row: TaskTableRow): void {
139	  selectedActiveRow.value = [row];
140	}
141	
142	function onActiveSelectionChange(selected: TaskTableRow[]): void {
143	  selectedActiveRow.value = selected;
144	}
145	
146	function onHistoryRowClick(row: HistoryTableRow): void {
147	  selectedHistoryRow.value = [row];
148	}
149	
150	function onHistorySelectionChange(selected: HistoryTableRow[]): void {
151	  selectedHistoryRow.value = selected;
152	}
153	
154	// ===== Task lifecycle actions =====
155	async function onStart(id: string): Promise<void> {
156	  await executeAction(`start-${id}`, async () => {
157	    taskService.startTask(id);
158	    notify.success('任务已启动');
159	    refreshLists();
160	  });
161	}
162	
163	async function onPause(id: string): Promise<void> {
164	  await executeAction(`pause-${id}`, async () => {
165	    taskService.pauseTask(id);
166	    notify.info('任务已暂停');
167	    refreshLists();
168	  });
169	}
170	
171	async function onResume(id: string): Promise<void> {
172	  await executeAction(`resume-${id}`, async () => {
173	    taskService.resumeTask(id);
174	    notify.success('任务已恢复');
175	    refreshLists();
176	  });
177	}
178	
179	function onStop(id: string): void {
180	  $q.dialog({
181	    title: '确认停止',
182	    message: '确定要停止该任务吗？',
183	    cancel: true,
184	    persistent: false,
185	  }).onOk(async () => {
186	    await executeAction(`stop-${id}`, async () => {
187	      taskService.stopTask(id);
188	      notify.info('任务已停止');
189	      refreshLists();
190	    });
191	  });
192	}
193	
194	async function onRemove(id: string): Promise<void> {
195	  $q.dialog({
196	    title: '确认删除',
197	    message: '确定要删除该任务吗？此操作不可撤销。',
198	    cancel: true,
199	    persistent: false,
200	  }).onOk(async () => {
201	    await executeAction(`remove-${id}`, async () => {
202	      taskService.removeTask(id);
203	      notify.success('任务已删除');
204	      selectedActiveRow.value = [];
205	      selectedHistoryRow.value = [];
206	      refreshLists();
207	    });
208	  });
209	}
210	
211	async function onRetry(id: string): Promise<void> {
212	  await executeAction(`retry-${id}`, async () => {
213	    const result = taskService.retryTask(id);
214	    if (result) {
215	      notify.success('任务已重新执行');
216	      refreshLists();
217	    } else {
218	      notify.error('重新执行失败');
219	    }
220	  });
221	}
222	
223	async function onStopAll(): Promise<void> {
224	  $q.dialog({
225	    title: '确认全部停止',
226	    message: '确定要停止所有运行中的任务吗？',
227	    cancel: true,
228	    persistent: false,
229	  }).onOk(async () => {
230	    await executeAction('stopAll', async () => {
231	      const count = taskService.stopAll();
232	      notify.info(`已停止 ${count} 个任务`);
233	      refreshLists();
234	    });
235	  });
236	}
237	
238	// ===== Editor dialog =====
239	let nextStepId = 1;
240	
241	function onNewTask(): void {
242	  editor.openNew();
243	}
244	
245	function onEditTask(): void {
246	  if (!selectedInstance.value) return;
247	  if (selectedInstance.value.lifecycle !== 'created') {
248	    notify.warning('只能编辑待启动状态的任务');
249	    return;
250	  }
251	  editor.openEdit(selectedInstance.value.instanceId);
252	}
253	
254	function onEditorHide(): void {
255	  editor.close();
256	}
257	
258	function onSave(): void {
259	  const id = editor.save();
260	  if (id) {
261	    notify.success('任务已保存');
262	    refreshLists();
263	  }
264	}
265	
266	function onSaveAndStart(): void {
267	  const id = editor.saveAndStart();
268	  if (id) {
269	    notify.success('任务已保存并启动');
270	    refreshLists();
271	  }
272	}
273	
274	function onAddStep(kind: string): void {
275	  let step: TaskStepDefinition;
276	  switch (kind) {
277	    case 'send':
278	      step = createSendStep(
279	        { frameId: '', targetId: '' },
280	        { id: `step-${nextStepId++}`, name: '发送步骤' },
281	      );
282	      break;
283	    case 'wait-condition':
284	      step = createWaitConditionStep(
285	        { conditions: [], onTimeout: 'fail' } as WaitConditionConfig,
286	        { id: `step-${nextStepId++}`, name: '等待条件' },
287	      );
288	      break;
289	    case 'delay':
290	      step = createDelayStep(1000, { id: `step-${nextStepId++}`, name: '延时' });
291	      break;
292	    default:
293	      return;
294	  }
295	  editor.addStep(step);
296	}
297	
298	// ===== Formatting helpers =====
299	function formatElapsed(ms: number): string {
300	  if (ms < 1000) return `${ms}ms`;
301	  const s = Math.floor(ms / 1000);
302	  if (s < 60) return `${s}s`;
303	  const m = Math.floor(s / 60);
304	  const rs = s % 60;
305	  if (m < 60) return `${m}m ${rs}s`;
306	  const h = Math.floor(m / 60);
307	  const rm = m % 60;
308	  return `${h}h ${rm}m`;
309	}
310	
311	function formatTime(iso?: string): string {
312	  if (!iso) return '--';
313	  const d = new Date(iso);
314	  return d.toLocaleTimeString('zh-CN', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit' });
315	}
316	
317	// ===== Step editor helpers =====
318	function getFrameFields(frameId: string) {
319	  if (!frameId) return [];
320	  const frame = frameService.getFrame(frameId);
321	  return frame?.fields ?? [];
322	}
323	
324	function updateSendStepField(index: number, key: keyof SendStepConfig, value: unknown): void {
325	  const step = editor.steps[index];
326	  if (step.kind !== 'send') return;
327	  const config = { ...step.config, [key]: value } as SendStepConfig;
328	  editor.updateStep(index, { ...step, config });
329	}
330	
331	function updateDelayStepDuration(index: number, durationMs: number): void {
332	  const step = editor.steps[index];
333	  if (step.kind !== 'delay') return;
334	  editor.updateStep(index, { ...step, config: { durationMs } });
335	}
336	
337	function updateWaitConditionConfig(index: number, config: WaitConditionConfig): void {
338	  const step = editor.steps[index];
339	  if (step.kind !== 'wait-condition') return;
340	  editor.updateStep(index, { ...step, config });
341	}
342	
343	function addWaitConditionTerm(stepIndex: number): void {
344	  const step = editor.steps[stepIndex];
345	  if (step.kind !== 'wait-condition') return;
346	  const config = step.config as WaitConditionConfig;
347	  const conditions = [...config.conditions, { frameId: '', fieldId: '', operator: 'eq' as const, threshold: '' }];
348	  updateWaitConditionConfig(stepIndex, { ...config, conditions });
349	}
350	
351	function removeWaitConditionTerm(stepIndex: number, termIndex: number): void {
352	  const step = editor.steps[stepIndex];
353	  if (step.kind !== 'wait-condition') return;
354	  const config = step.config as WaitConditionConfig;
355	  const conditions = config.conditions.filter((_, i) => i !== termIndex);
356	  updateWaitConditionConfig(stepIndex, { ...config, conditions });
357	}
358	
359	function updateWaitConditionTerm(stepIndex: number, termIndex: number, term: ConditionTerm): void {
360	  const step = editor.steps[stepIndex];
361	  if (step.kind !== 'wait-condition') return;
362	  const config = step.config as WaitConditionConfig;
363	  const conditions = config.conditions.map((c, i) => (i === termIndex ? term : c));
364	  updateWaitConditionConfig(stepIndex, { ...config, conditions });
365	}
366	</script>

735	                </div>

765	                dense

769	                header-class="rw-text-value text-sm"
770	              >

772	                  <!-- Send step -->

775	                      :model-value="step.name"
776	                      dense outlined

779	                    />

782	                      <q-select
783	                        :model-value="step.config.frameId"
784	                        :options="frameService.listFrames().map(f => ({ value: f.id, label: f.name }))"
785	                        emit-value map-options
786	                        outlined dense clearable
787	                        class="mt-1"
788	                        @update:model-value="updateSendStepField(si, 'frameId', $event ?? '')"
789	                      />
790	                    </div>
791	                    <div>
792	                      <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">发送目标</span>
793	                      <SendTargetSelector
794	                        :model-value="step.config.targetId"
795	                        :connection-service="connectionService"
796	                        class="mt-1"
797	                        @update:model-value="updateSendStepField(si, 'targetId', $event ?? '')"
798	                      />
799	                    </div>
800	                    <template v-if="step.config.frameId && getFrameFields(step.config.frameId).length > 0">
801	                      <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">字段值</span>
802	                      <FieldEditWidget
803	                        :fields="getFrameFields(step.config.frameId)"
804	                        :values="step.config.userFieldValues ?? {}"
805	                        direction="send"
806	                        @update:values="updateSendStepField(si, 'userFieldValues', $event)"
807	                      />
808	                    </template>
809	                    <q-input
810	                      :model-value="step.config.intervalAfterMs"
811	                      dense outlined
812	                      type="number"
813	                      label="发送后延时 (ms)"
814	                      @update:model-value="updateSendStepField(si, 'intervalAfterMs', Number($event) || undefined)"
815	                    />
816	                    <q-btn
817	                      v-if="si > 0 && editor.steps.value[si - 1]?.kind === 'send'"
818	                      flat dense no-caps
819	                      label="复制上一步字段值"
820	                      size="sm"
821	                      color="primary"
822	                      @click="editor.duplicateStepValuesFromPrevious(si)"
823	                    />
824	                  </template>
825	
826	                  <!-- Wait condition step -->
827	                  <template v-if="step.kind === 'wait-condition'">
828	                    <q-input
829	                      :model-value="step.name"
830	                      dense outlined
831	                      label="步骤名称"
832	                      @update:model-value="editor.updateStep(si, { ...step, name: $event ?? '' })"
833	                    />
834	                    <div class="flex flex-col gap-2">
835	                      <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
836	                        <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">等待条件</span>
837	                        <q-btn flat dense no-caps icon="o_add" label="添加" size="sm" color="primary" @click="addWaitConditionTerm(si)" />
838	                      </div>
839	                      <div
840	                        v-for="(term, ti) in step.config.conditions"
841	                        :key="ti"
842	                        class="flex items-center gap-2"
843	                      >
844	                        <q-input
845	                          :model-value="String(term.threshold)"
846	                          outlined dense
847	                          placeholder="阈值"
848	                          class="flex-1"
849	                          @update:model-value="updateWaitConditionTerm(si, ti, { ...term, threshold: $event })"
850	                        />
851	                        <q-btn flat round dense icon="o_close" size="xs" color="negative" @click="removeWaitConditionTerm(si, ti)" />
852	                      </div>
853	                      <div v-if="step.config.conditions.length === 0" class="rw-text-desc text-xs">至少添加一条条件</div>
854	                    </div>
855	                    <q-input
856	                      :model-value="step.config.timeoutMs"
857	                      dense outlined
858	                      type="number"
859	                      label="超时时间 (ms)"
860	                      :rules="[val => !val || val > 0 || '超时时间必须大于0']"
861	                      @update:model-value="updateWaitConditionConfig(si, { ...step.config, timeoutMs: Number($event) || undefined })"
862	                    />
863	                    <div>
864	                      <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">超时策略</span>
865	                      <q-select
866	                        :model-value="step.config.onTimeout"
867	                        :options="ON_TIMEOUT_OPTIONS"
868	                        emit-value map-options
869	                        outlined dense
870	                        class="mt-1"
871	                        @update:model-value="updateWaitConditionConfig(si, { ...step.config, onTimeout: $event as WaitConditionConfig['onTimeout'] })"
872	                      />
873	                    </div>
874	                  </template>
875	
876	                  <!-- Delay step -->
877	                  <template v-if="step.kind === 'delay'">
878	                    <q-input
879	                      :model-value="step.name"
880	                      dense outlined
881	                      label="步骤名称"
882	                      @update:model-value="editor.updateStep(si, { ...step, name: $event ?? '' })"
883	                    />
884	                    <q-input
885	                      :model-value="step.config.durationMs"
886	                      dense outlined
887	                      type="number"
888	                      label="持续时间 (ms)"
889	                      :rules="[val => val > 0 || '延时时间必须大于0']"
890	                      @update:model-value="updateDelayStepDuration(si, Number($event) || 1000)"
891	                    />
892	                  </template>
893	
894	                  <!-- Remove step -->
895	                  <q-btn
896	                    flat dense no-caps
897	                    label="删除步骤"
898	                    icon="o_delete"
899	                    size="sm"
900	                    color="negative"
901	                    @click="editor.removeStep(si)"
902	                  />
903	                </div>
904	              </q-expansion-item>
905	
906	              <div v-if="editor.steps.value.length === 0" class="rw-text-desc text-xs text-center q-pa-sm">
907	                点击上方按钮添加步骤
908	              </div>
909	            </div>
910	
911	            <q-separator />
912	
913	            <!-- Advanced config -->
914	            <q-expansion-item dense label="高级配置" header-class="rw-text-label text-sm">
915	              <div class="q-pl-md flex flex-col gap-3">
916	                <q-input
917	                  :model-value="editor.stopCondition.value.maxIterations"
918	                  dense outlined
919	                  type="number"
920	                  label="最大迭代次数"
921	                  @update:model-value="editor.stopCondition.value = { ...editor.stopCondition.value, maxIterations: Number($event) || undefined }"
922	                />
923	                <q-input
924	                  :model-value="editor.stopCondition.value.maxDurationMs"
925	                  dense outlined
926	                  type="number"
927	                  label="最大持续时间 (ms)"

929	                />
930	                <div>

935	                    emit-value map-options
936	                    outlined dense
937	                    class="mt-1"
938	                    @update:model-value="editor.errorPolicy.value = { ...editor.errorPolicy.value, onFailure: $event }"
939	                  />
940	                </div>
941	                <q-input
942	                  :model-value="editor.errorPolicy.value.retryCount"
943	                  dense outlined
944	                  type="number"
945	                  label="重试次数"
946	                  :disable="editor.errorPolicy.value.onFailure !== 'retry'"
947	                  @update:model-value="editor.errorPolicy.value = { ...editor.errorPolicy.value, retryCount: Number($event) || undefined }"
948	                />
949	                <q-input
950	                  :model-value="editor.errorPolicy.value.retryDelayMs"
951	                  dense outlined
952	                  type="number"
953	                  label="重试间隔 (ms)"
954	                  :disable="editor.errorPolicy.value.onFailure !== 'retry'"
955	                  @update:model-value="editor.errorPolicy.value = { ...editor.errorPolicy.value, retryDelayMs: Number($event) || undefined }"
956	                />
957	                <div class="rw-text-desc text-xs">

962	
963	            <!-- Validation issues -->

}

  "content": "export function hexToBytes(hex: string): number[] {\n  const cleaned = hex.replace(/\\s/g, '');\n  if (cleaned.length % 2 !== 0) {\n    throw new Error('Hex string must have even length');\n  }\n  const bytes: number[] = [];\n  for (let i = 0; i < cleaned.length; i += 2) {\n    bytes.push(parseInt(cleaned.substring(i, i + 2), 16));\n  }\n  return bytes;\n}\n"
}

21	const $q = useQuasar();
22	const notify = useNotify();
23	const runtime = useRewriteRuntime();
24	const service = runtime.features.commandIngressService;
25	
26	// ===== Tab state =====
27	const activeTab = ref('monitor');
28	
29	// ===== Composables =====
30	const scoeConfig = useScoeConfig();
31	const monitor = useScoeMonitor(service);
32	const testTool = useTestTool(service, () => '');
33	const docking = useCentralDocking();
34	const taskReport = useTaskReport();
35	
36	// ===== Actions =====
37	const { execute, isOperating } = useAsyncAction();
38	
39	// ===== Polling =====
40	function refreshAll(): void {
41	  monitor.refresh();
42	  testTool.refreshRecords();
43	}
44	
45	const polling = usePolling(refreshAll, 1000);
46	
47	// ===== Highlight dialog =====
48	const editingHighlightRules = shallowRef<HighlightRuleConfig[]>([]);
49	
50	// ===== Title bar actions =====
51	async function handleConnect(): Promise<void> {
52	  await execute('connect', async () => {
53	    notify.success('已连接');
54	  });
55	}
56	
57	async function handleDisconnect(): Promise<void> {
58	  await execute('disconnect', async () => {
59	    notify.success('已断开');
60	  });
61	}
62	
63	async function handleLoadSatellite(): Promise<void> {
64	  const satelliteId = scoeConfig.selectedConfigId.value;
65	  if (!satelliteId) return;
66	  await execute('load', async () => {
67	    const result = await service.loadSatellite(satelliteId);
68	    if (result.success) {
69	      notify.success(`卫星 ${satelliteId} 已加载`);
70	    } else {
71	      notify.error('加载失败', result.error);
72	    }
73	  });
74	}
75	
76	async function handleUnloadSatellite(): Promise<void> {
77	  await execute('unload', async () => {
78	    const result = await service.unloadSatellite();
79	    if (result.success) {
80	      notify.success('已卸载卫星');
81	    } else {
82	      notify.error('卸载失败', result.error);
83	    }
84	  });
85	}
86	
87	async function handleSaveConfig(): Promise<void> {
88	  await execute('save-config', async () => {
89	    await scoeConfig.saveConfigs();
90	    notify.success('配置已保存');
91	  });
92	}
93	
94	// ===== SCOE Config tab actions =====
95	function handleDeleteSatellite(satelliteId: string): void {
96	  $q.dialog({
97	    title: '确认删除',
98	    message: `确定要删除卫星配置「${satelliteId}」吗？此操作不可恢复。`,
99	    cancel: true,
100	    persistent: false,
101	  }).onOk(() => {
102	    scoeConfig.removeSatellite(satelliteId);
103	  });
104	}
105	
106	function handleDuplicateSatellite(satelliteId: string): void {
107	  scoeConfig.duplicateSatellite(satelliteId);
108	}
109	
110	// ===== Test tool actions =====
111	async function handleSendHex(): Promise<void> {
112	  await execute('send-hex', async () => {
113	    await testTool.sendHex();
114	    notify.success('数据已发送');
115	  });
116	}
117	
118	function handleClearTestRecords(): void {
119	  $q.dialog({
120	    title: '确认清空',
121	    message: '确定要清空所有接收数据吗？此操作不可恢复。',
122	    cancel: true,
123	    persistent: false,
124	  }).onOk(() => {
125	    testTool.clearRecords();
126	  });
127	}
128	
129	function handleClearCommandLog(): void {
130	  $q.dialog({
131	    title: '确认清空',
132	    message: '确定要清空所有命令记录吗？此操作不可恢复。',
133	    cancel: true,
134	    persistent: false,
135	  }).onOk(() => {
136	    monitor.clearCommandLog();
137	  });
138	}
139	
140	function handleSaveHighlightRules(rules: HighlightRuleConfig[]): void {
141	  testTool.saveHighlightRules(rules);
142	  testTool.showHighlightDialog.value = false;
143	  notify.success('高亮规则已保存');
144	}
145	
146	function handleDeleteHighlightRule(ruleId: string): void {
147	  $q.dialog({
148	    title: '确认删除',
149	    message: '确定要删除此高亮规则吗？',
150	    cancel: true,
151	    persistent: false,
152	  }).onOk(() => {
153	    editingHighlightRules.value = editingHighlightRules.value.filter(
154	      (r) => r.id !== ruleId,
155	    );
156	  });
157	}
158	
159	function openHighlightDialog(): void {
160	  editingHighlightRules.value = [...testTool.highlightRules.value];
161	  testTool.showHighlightDialog.value = true;
162	}
163	
164	function addHighlightRule(): void {
165	  editingHighlightRules.value = [
166	    ...editingHighlightRules.value,
167	    { id: `rule_${Date.now()}`, offset: 0, length: 1, severity: 'info' },
168	  ];
169	}
170	
171	// ===== Stats panel helpers =====
172	function getStatsDisplay(key: string): string | number {
173	  const stats = monitor.statistics.value;
174	  const status = monitor.runtimeStatus.value;
175	  switch (key) {
176	    case 'runtimeSeconds': return stats.runtimeSeconds || '—';
177	    case 'satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds': return stats.satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds || '—';
178	    case 'commandReceiveCount': return stats.commandReceiveCount;
179	    case 'commandSuccessCount': return stats.commandSuccessCount;
180	    case 'commandErrorCount': return stats.commandErrorCount;
181	    case 'lastCommandCode': return status.lastCommandCode || '—';
182	    case 'lastErrorReason': return stats.lastErrorReason || '—';
183	    case 'loadedSatelliteId': return status.loadedSatelliteId || '—';
184	    default: return '—';
185	  }
186	}
187	
188	function isStatusKey(key: string): boolean {
189	  return key === 'healthStatus' || key === 'linkTestResult';
190	}
191	
192	function getStatusMap(key: string): Record<string, { label: string; color: string }> {
193	  return key === 'healthStatus' ? healthStatusMap : linkTestStatusMap;
194	}
195	
196	function getStatusValue(key: string): string {
197	  const status = monitor.runtimeStatus.value;
198	  return key === 'healthStatus' ? status.healthStatus : status.linkTestResult;
199	}
200	
201	// ===== Config search =====
202	const configSearch = ref('');
203	
204	// ===== Format helpers =====
205	function formatCommandCode(code: number): string {
206	  return `0x${code.toString(16).toUpperCase().padStart(2, '0')}`;
207	}
208	
209	function formatDuration(ms: number | undefined | null): string {
210	  if (ms === undefined || ms === null) return '—';
211	  return `${ms} ms`;
212	}
213	
214	// ===== Lifecycle =====
215	onMounted(() => {
216	  scoeConfig.loadConfigs();
217	  polling.start();
218	});
219	
220	onBeforeUnmount(() => {
221	  polling.stop();
222	});
223	</script>

44	  void refreshKey.value;
45	  return frameService.listFrames({
46	    query: searchText.value || undefined,
47	    direction: directionFilter.value || undefined,
48	    favoriteOnly: favoriteOnly.value || undefined,
49	  });
50	});
51	
52	interface TableRow {
53	  readonly id: string;
54	  readonly name: string;
55	  readonly direction: FrameDirection;
56	  readonly fieldCount: number;
57	  readonly description?: string;
58	  readonly frameType?: string;
59	  readonly isFavorite: boolean;
60	  readonly _index: number;
61	}
62	
63	const tableRows = shallowRef<TableRow[]>([]);
64	const selectedRows = shallowRef<TableRow[]>([]);
65	
66	watch(frameList, (list) => {
67	  tableRows.value = list.map((frame, index) => ({ ...frame, _index: index + 1 }));
68	}, { immediate: true });
69	
70	watch([tableRows, selectedFrameIds], ([rows, ids]) => {
71	  selectedRows.value = rows.filter((row) => ids.includes(row.id));
72	}, { immediate: true });
73	
74	const detailFrame = computed<ReadonlyFrameAsset | null>(() => {
75	  if (selectedFrameIds.value.length !== 1) return null;
76	  return frameService.getFrame(selectedFrameIds.value[0]) ?? null;
77	});
78	
79	// --- Operations ---
80	
81	function onRowClick(row: TableRow): void {
82	  selectedFrameIds.value = [row.id];
83	  showDetailPanel.value = true;
84	  frameService.selectFrame(row.id);
85	}
86	
87	function onSelectionChange(selected: TableRow[]): void {
88	  selectedFrameIds.value = selected.map((r) => r.id);
89	}
90	
91	function toggleFavorite(frame: FrameAssetSummary): void {
92	  const full = frameService.getFrame(frame.id);
93	  if (!full) return;
94	  const cloned = cloneFrameAsset(full);
95	  cloned.isFavorite = !cloned.isFavorite;
96	  const result = frameService.upsertFrame(cloned);
97	  if (result.ok) {
98	    refreshList();
99	  } else {
100	    $q.notify({ type: 'negative', message: '操作失败' });
101	  }
102	}
103	
104	function cloneFrame(frame: FrameAssetSummary): void {
105	  const full = frameService.getFrame(frame.id);
106	  if (!full) return;
107	  const cloned = cloneFrameAsset(full);
108	  cloned.id = `${cloned.id}_copy`;
109	  cloned.name = `${cloned.name} (副本)`;
110	  const result = frameService.upsertFrame(cloned);
111	  if (result.ok) {
112	    refreshList();
113	    $q.notify({ type: 'positive', message: '已复制' });
114	  } else {
115	    $q.notify({ type: 'negative', message: '复制失败' });
116	  }

119	function removeFrame(frameId: string): void {
120	  $q.dialog({
121	    title: '确认删除',
122	    message: '确定要删除这个帧定义吗？此操作不可撤销。',
123	    cancel: true,
124	    persistent: true,
125	  }).onOk(() => {
126	    const result = frameService.removeFrame(frameId);
127	    if (result.ok) {
128	      selectedFrameIds.value = selectedFrameIds.value.filter((id) => id !== frameId);
129	      refreshList();
130	      $q.notify({ type: 'positive', message: '已删除' });
131	    }
132	  });
133	}
134	
135	function onImportConfirm(frames: FrameAsset[]): void {
136	  const result = frameService.replaceFrames(frames);
137	  if (result.ok) {
138	    refreshList();
139	    $q.notify({ type: 'positive', message: `成功导入 ${frames.length} 个帧定义` });
140	  } else {
141	    $q.notify({ type: 'negative', message: '导入失败' });
142	  }
143	}
144	
145	async function onExport(): Promise<void> {
146	  const allFrames = frameService.findFrames();
147	  const json = serializeFrames(allFrames);
148	
149	  const fileFacade = getFileFacade();
150	  if (fileFacade) {
151	    const path = await fileFacade.showSaveDialog({
152	      title: '导出帧定义',
153	      defaultPath: 'frames.json',
154	      filters: [{ name: 'JSON', extensions: ['json'] }],
155	    });
156	    if (path) {
157	      await fileFacade.writeTextFile(path, json);
158	      $q.notify({ type: 'positive', message: '导出成功' });
159	    }
160	  } else {
161	    const blob = new Blob([json], { type: 'application/json' });
162	    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
163	    const a = document.createElement('a');
164	    a.href = url;
165	    a.download = 'frames.json';
166	    a.click();
167	    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
168	    $q.notify({ type: 'positive', message: '已下载' });
169	  }
170	}
171	
172	function onCloseDetail(): void {
173	  showDetailPanel.value = false;
174	}
175	
176	function onEditFrame(frameId: string): void {
177	  if (frameId === 'new') {
178	    router.push('/frames/editor');
179	  } else {
180	    router.push(`/frames/editor/${frameId}`);
181	  }
182	}
183	</script>

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { computed, ref, shallowRef, watch } from 'vue';
3	import { useQuasar } from 'quasar';
4	import { useRewriteRuntime } from '@/app/rewriteRuntime';
5	import DataTable from '@/widgets/DataTable.vue';
6	import FieldEditWidget from '@/widgets/FieldEditWidget.vue';
7	import SendTargetSelector from '@/features/send/components/SendTargetSelector.vue';
8	import { instanceColumns, type InstanceTableRow } from '@/features/send/components/instance-columns';
9	import { useSendInstances } from '@/features/send/composables/use-send-instances';
10	import { useFramePreview } from '@/features/send/composables/use-frame-preview';
11	import { useAsyncAction, useNotify } from '@/shared/composables';
12	import { listFrameAssetSummaries, cloneFrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';
13	import type { ReadonlyFrameAsset, FrameAssetSummary } from '@/features/frame';
14	import type { SendFieldValue, SendFrameInstance } from '@/features/send';
15	
16	const $q = useQuasar();
17	const notify = useNotify();
18	const { execute: executeAction, isOperating } = useAsyncAction();
19	const runtime = useRewriteRuntime();
20	const frameService = runtime.features.frameService;
21	const sendService = runtime.features.sendService;
22	const connectionService = runtime.features.connectionService;
23	
24	const sendInstances = useSendInstances();
25	
26	// ===== Left column: frame list =====
27	
28	const searchText = ref('');
29	const favoriteOnly = ref(false);
30	
31	// Reactive refresh — service state is non-reactive, track mutations manually
32	const refreshKey = ref(0);
33	function refreshFrameList(): void {
34	  refreshKey.value++;
35	}
36	
37	const frameSummaries = computed<FrameAssetSummary[]>(() => {
38	  void refreshKey.value;
39	  return listFrameAssetSummaries(frameService.getSnapshot(), {
40	    direction: 'send',
41	    query: searchText.value || undefined,
42	    favoriteOnly: favoriteOnly.value || undefined,
43	  });
44	});
45	
46	const favoriteFrames = computed(() =>
47	  frameSummaries.value.filter((f) => f.isFavorite),
48	);
49	
50	const otherFrames = computed(() =>
51	  frameSummaries.value.filter((f) => !f.isFavorite),
52	);
53	
54	function getDefaultFieldValues(frame: ReadonlyFrameAsset): Record<string, SendFieldValue> {
55	  const values: Record<string, SendFieldValue> = {};
56	  for (const field of frame.fields) {
57	    if (!field.configurable) continue;
58	    if (field.defaultValue !== undefined) {
59	      const num = Number(field.defaultValue);
60	      values[field.id] = !Number.isNaN(num) && field.defaultValue.trim() !== '' ? num : field.defaultValue;
61	    } else if (field.options.length > 0) {
62	      const defaultOpt = field.options.find(o => o.isDefault);
63	      values[field.id] = defaultOpt ? Number(defaultOpt.value) || defaultOpt.value : Number(field.options[0].value) || field.options[0].value;
64	    }
65	  }
66	  return values;
67	}
68	
69	function onFrameClick(frame: FrameAssetSummary): void {
70	  const fullFrame = frameService.getFrame(frame.id);
71	  if (!fullFrame) return;
72	  const defaultValues = getDefaultFieldValues(fullFrame);
73	  const instance = sendInstances.createInstance(frame.id, frame.name, defaultValues);
74	  sendInstances.selectInstance(instance.instanceId);
75	}
76	
77	function toggleFavorite(frame: FrameAssetSummary): void {
78	  const full = frameService.getFrame(frame.id);
79	  if (!full) return;
80	  const cloned = cloneFrameAsset(full);
81	  cloned.isFavorite = !cloned.isFavorite;
82	  const result = frameService.upsertFrame(cloned);
83	  if (result.ok) {
84	    refreshFrameList();
85	  } else {
86	    notify.error('操作失败');
87	  }
88	}
89	
90	// ===== Middle column: instance table =====
91	
92	const tableRows = shallowRef<InstanceTableRow[]>([]);
93	const selectedRows = shallowRef<InstanceTableRow[]>([]);
94	
95	watch(sendInstances.instances, (instances) => {
96	  tableRows.value = instances.map((inst, index) => ({
97	    instanceId: inst.instanceId,
98	    name: inst.name,
99	    fieldCount: Object.keys(inst.userFieldValues).length,
100	    sendCount: inst.sendCount,
101	    lastSendAt: inst.lastSendAt,
102	    description: inst.description,
103	    _index: index + 1,
104	  }));
105	}, { immediate: true });
106	
107	watch([tableRows, sendInstances.selectedInstanceId], ([rows, id]) => {
108	  selectedRows.value = id ? rows.filter((r) => r.instanceId === id) : [];
109	}, { immediate: true });
110	
111	function onRowClick(row: InstanceTableRow): void {
112	  sendInstances.selectInstance(row.instanceId);
113	}
114	
115	function onSelectionChange(selected: InstanceTableRow[]): void {
116	  if (selected.length > 0) {
117	    sendInstances.selectInstance(selected[0].instanceId);
118	  } else {
119	    sendInstances.selectInstance(null);
120	  }
121	}
122	
123	function onEditInstance(row: InstanceTableRow): void {
124	  const inst = sendInstances.instances.value.find(i => i.instanceId === row.instanceId);
125	  if (!inst) return;
126	  editingInstanceId.value = inst.instanceId;
127	  editValues.value = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(inst.userFieldValues));
128	  editDescription.value = inst.description ?? '';
129	  editName.value = inst.name;
130	  showEditDialog.value = true;
131	}
132	
133	function onCloneInstance(row: InstanceTableRow): void {
134	  const cloned = sendInstances.cloneInstance(row.instanceId);
135	  if (cloned) {
136	    notify.success('已复制');
137	  }
138	}
139	
140	function onRemoveInstance(row: InstanceTableRow): void {
141	  $q.dialog({
142	    title: '确认删除',
143	    message: `确定要删除实例「${row.name}」吗？此操作不可撤销。`,
144	    cancel: true,
145	    persistent: false,
146	  }).onOk(() => {
147	    sendInstances.removeInstance(row.instanceId);
148	  });
149	}
150	
151	function onMoveUp(row: InstanceTableRow): void {
152	  sendInstances.moveInstanceUp(row.instanceId);
153	}
154	
155	function onMoveDown(row: InstanceTableRow): void {
156	  sendInstances.moveInstanceDown(row.instanceId);
157	}
158	
159	// ===== Right column: preview + send =====
160	
161	const selectedInstance = computed<SendFrameInstance | null>(() => {
162	  const id = sendInstances.selectedInstanceId.value;
163	  if (!id) return null;
164	  return sendInstances.instances.value.find(i => i.instanceId === id) ?? null;
165	});
166	
167	const selectedFrame = computed<ReadonlyFrameAsset | null>(() => {
168	  if (!selectedInstance.value) return null;
169	  return frameService.getFrame(selectedInstance.value.frameId) ?? null;
170	});
171	
172	const selectedValues = computed<Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>>(() => {
173	  return selectedInstance.value?.userFieldValues ?? {};
174	});
175	
176	const { fullPreview } = useFramePreview(selectedFrame, selectedValues);
177	
178	const selectedTargetId = ref<string | null>(null);
179	
180	async function onSend(): Promise<void> {
181	  const inst = selectedInstance.value;
182	  if (!inst || !selectedTargetId.value) return;
183	
184	  await executeAction('send', async () => {
185	    const result = await sendService.execute({
186	      frameId: inst.frameId,
187	      targetId: selectedTargetId.value!,
188	      userFieldValues: inst.userFieldValues,
189	      context: { source: 'user' },
190	    });
191	
192	    if (result.kind === 'sent') {
193	      notify.success('发送成功');
194	      sendInstances.incrementSendCount(inst.instanceId);
195	    } else {
196	      notify.error('发送失败', result.error?.message);
197	      if (result.buildIssues.length > 0) {
198	        const msgs = result.buildIssues
199	          .filter(i => i.severity === 'error')
200	          .map(i => i.message)
201	          .join('; ');
202	        if (msgs) {
203	          notify.error('构建问题', msgs);
204	        }
205	      }
206	    }
207	  });
208	}
209	
210	// ===== Instance edit dialog =====
211	
212	const showEditDialog = ref(false);
213	const editingInstanceId = ref<string | null>(null);
214	const editValues = ref<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>({});
215	const editDescription = ref('');
216	const editName = ref('');
217	const isSaving = ref(false);
218	
219	const isValid = computed(() => {
220	  return editName.value.trim().length > 0;
221	});
222	
223	function onEditDialogHide(): void {
224	  editingInstanceId.value = null;
225	  editValues.value = {};
226	  editDescription.value = '';
227	  editName.value = '';
228	}
229	
230	async function onEditConfirm(): Promise<void> {
231	  if (!editingInstanceId.value || !isValid.value) return;
232	
233	  isSaving.value = true;
234	  try {
235	    sendInstances.updateInstance(editingInstanceId.value, {
236	      name: editName.value,
237	      description: editDescription.value,
238	      userFieldValues: { ...editValues.value },
239	    });
240	    showEditDialog.value = false;
241	  } finally {
242	    isSaving.value = false;
243	  }
244	}
245	
246	// Configurable fields of selected frame for right panel display
247	const configurableFields = computed(() => {
248	  const frame = selectedFrame.value;
249	  if (!frame) return [];
250	  return frame.fields.filter(f => f.configurable);
251	});
252	
253	// Edit dialog: frame fields for the instance being edited
254	const editFrame = computed<ReadonlyFrameAsset | null>(() => {
255	  if (!editingInstanceId.value) return null;
256	  const inst = sendInstances.instances.value.find(i => i.instanceId === editingInstanceId.value);
257	  if (!inst) return null;
258	  return frameService.getFrame(inst.frameId) ?? null;
259	});
260	
261	const editFrameFields = computed(() => editFrame.value?.fields ?? []);
262	</script>
