```
1	import { ref, shallowRef } from 'vue';
2	import type { TaskService } from '../services';
3	import type { TaskInstanceState, TaskProgress } from '../core';
4	import { isTerminal, calculateProgress } from '../core';
5	import { resolveDisplayStatus } from '../components/taskStatusMap';
6	import { TASK_STATUS_MAP } from '../components/taskStatusMap';
7	import { usePolling } from '@/shared/composables';
8	
9	export function useTaskMonitor(taskService: TaskService) {
10	  const selectedId = ref<string | null>(null);
11	  const instance = shallowRef<TaskInstanceState | undefined>(undefined);
12	  const progress = shallowRef<TaskProgress | null>(null);
13	  const displayStatus = ref('');
14	  const statusInfo = shallowRef<{ label: string; color: string }>({
15	    label: '',
16	    color: 'grey',
17	  });
18	
19	  const polling = usePolling(refresh, 1000);
20	
21	  function refresh(): void {
22	    if (!selectedId.value) return;
23	    const inst = taskService.getInstance(selectedId.value);
24	    if (!inst) {
25	      instance.value = undefined;
26	      progress.value = null;
27	      displayStatus.value = '';
28	      statusInfo.value = { label: '', color: 'grey' };
29	      return;
30	    }
31	    instance.value = inst;
32	    progress.value = calculateProgress(inst);
33	    const status = resolveDisplayStatus(inst.lifecycle, inst.definitionRef);
34	    displayStatus.value = status;
35	    statusInfo.value = TASK_STATUS_MAP[status] ?? { label: status, color: 'grey' };
36	
37	    if (isTerminal(inst.lifecycle)) {
38	      polling.stop();
39	    }
40	  }
41	
42	  function select(instanceId: string | null): void {
43	    selectedId.value = instanceId;
44	    if (instanceId) {
45	      refresh();
46	      const inst = taskService.getInstance(instanceId);
47	      if (inst && !isTerminal(inst.lifecycle)) {
48	        polling.start();
49	      }
50	    } else {
51	      instance.value = undefined;
52	      progress.value = null;
53	      displayStatus.value = '';
54	      statusInfo.value = { label: '', color: 'grey' };
55	      polling.stop();
56	    }
57	  }
58	
59	  return {
60	    selectedId,
61	    instance,
62	    progress,
63	    displayStatus,
64	    statusInfo,
65	    polling,
66	    select,
67	    refresh,
68	  };
69	}
70
```

```
1	import { defaultNow } from '@/shared';
2	import { buildFrame, applyBuildPostPatch } from '../core';
3	import { frameToBuildInput, resolveFieldValues, applyFactor } from '../core/frame-resolver';
4	import type {
5	  ReadonlySendStateSnapshot,
6	  SendBuildIssue,
7	  SendError,
8	  SendRequest,
9	  SendRequestRef,
10	  SendResult,
11	  SendResultKind,
12	  SendServiceStatus,
13	  SendStateSnapshot,
14	  SendStatisticsSnapshot,
15	} from '../core';
16	import type {
17	  SendFrameReader,
18	  SendResultEmitter,
19	  SendTargetResolver,
20	  SendTransportWriteOutcome,
21	  SendTransportWriter,
22	  SendVariableProvider,
23	} from '../adapters';
24	import { createSendState, type SendStateContainer } from '../state/send-state';
25	
26	export interface SendReader {
27	  getSnapshot(): SendStateSnapshot;
28	  getStatistics(): SendStatisticsSnapshot;
29	  listResults(): SendResult[];
30	  getStatus(): SendServiceStatus;
31	}
32	
33	export interface SendService extends SendReader {
34	  execute(request: SendRequest): Promise<SendResult>;
35	  resetStats(): SendStateSnapshot;
36	}
37	
38	export interface CreateSendServiceOptions {
39	  readonly frameReader: SendFrameReader;
40	  readonly targetResolver: SendTargetResolver;
41	  readonly transportWriter: SendTransportWriter;
42	  readonly variableProvider?: SendVariableProvider;
43	  readonly resultEmitter?: SendResultEmitter;
44	  readonly state?: SendStateContainer;
45	  readonly now?: () => string;
46	}
47	
48	function validateSendRequest(request: SendRequest): SendBuildIssue[] {
49	  const issues: SendBuildIssue[] = [];
50	  if (!request.frameId) {
51	    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'send.request.missingFrameId', message: 'frameId is required.' });
52	  }
53	  if (!request.targetId) {
54	    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'send.request.missingTargetId', message: 'targetId is required.' });
55	  }
56	  if (!request.context?.source) {
57	    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'send.request.missingSource', message: 'context.source is required.' });
58	  }
59	  return issues;
60	}
61	
62	function toRequestRef(request: SendRequest): SendRequestRef {
63	  return {
64	    frameId: request.frameId,
65	    targetId: request.targetId,
66	    context: { ...request.context },
67	  };
68	}
69	
70	function makeResult(
71	  kind: SendResultKind,
72	  requestRef: SendRequestRef,
73	  timestamp: string,
74	  bytesBuilt: number,
75	  bytesSent: number,
76	  error?: SendError,
77	  buildIssues: readonly SendBuildIssue[] = [],
78	): SendResult {
79	  return {
80	    kind,
81	    requestRef,
82	    bytesBuilt,
83	    bytesSent,
84	    timestamp,
85	    ...(error ? { error } : {}),
86	    buildIssues,
87	  };
88	}
89	
90	export function createSendReader(
91	  snapshotProvider: () => ReadonlySendStateSnapshot,
92	): SendReader {
93	  return {
94	    getSnapshot() {
95	      return structuredClone(snapshotProvider());
96	    },
97	    getStatistics() {
98	      return structuredClone(snapshotProvider().statistics);
99	    },
100	    listResults() {
101	      return structuredClone(snapshotProvider().recentResults);
102	    },
103	    getStatus() {
104	      return snapshotProvider().status;
105	    },
106	  };
107	}
108	
109	function noOpVariableProvider(): SendVariableProvider {
110	  return { getVariables: () => new Map() };
111	}
112	
113	export function createSendService(options: CreateSendServiceOptions): SendService {
114	  const state = options.state ?? createSendState();
115	  const now = options.now ?? defaultNow;
116	  const reader = createSendReader(() => state.getSnapshot());
117	  const variableProvider = options.variableProvider ?? noOpVariableProvider();
118	
119	  return {
120	    ...reader,
121	
122	    async execute(request) {
123	      const requestRef = toRequestRef(request);
124	      const timestamp = now();
125	
126	      state.setStatus('sending');
127	
128	      // 1. Validate request
129	      const validationIssues = validateSendRequest(request);
130	      const hasErrors = validationIssues.some((i) => i.severity === 'error');
131	      if (hasErrors) {
132	        const result = makeResult('build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0, undefined, validationIssues);
133	        state.addResult(result);
134	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
135	        return result;
136	      }
137	
138	      // 2. Resolve frame snapshot
139	      const frame = options.frameReader.getFrame(request.frameId);
140	      if (!frame) {
141	        const result = makeResult(
142	          'build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0,
143	          { kind: 'frame-not-found', message: `Frame not found: ${request.frameId}` },
144	        );
145	        state.addResult(result);
146	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
147	        return result;
148	      }
149	
150	      // 3. Direction check
151	      if (frame.direction === 'receive') {
152	        const result = makeResult(
153	          'build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0,
154	          { kind: 'wrong-direction', message: `Frame "${request.frameId}" is receive-only.` },
155	        );
156	        state.addResult(result);
157	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
158	        return result;
159	      }
160	
161	      // 4. Convert frame to encoding defs and resolve field values
162	      const { fields, totalByteLength } = frameToBuildInput(frame);
163	      const userValues = request.userFieldValues ?? request.fieldValues ?? {};
164	      const resolved = resolveFieldValues(fields, userValues, variableProvider);
165	      const factored = applyFactor(fields, resolved.values);
166	      const allIssues = [...resolved.issues, ...factored.issues];
167	
168	      // 5. Build frame bytes
169	      const buildOutput = buildFrame({
170	        fields,
171	        totalByteLength,
172	        fieldValues: factored.values,
173	      });
174	      allIssues.push(...buildOutput.issues);
175	
176	      const buildErrors = allIssues.filter((i) => i.severity === 'error');
177	      if (buildErrors.length > 0) {
178	        const result = makeResult('build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0, undefined, allIssues);
179	        state.addResult(result);
180	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
181	        return result;
182	      }
183	
184	      let bytes = buildOutput.bytes;
185	
186	      // 6. Apply post-build patch (checksum, length)
187	      const frameOptions = frame.options;
188	      const patchOptions = request.options ?? (frameOptions ? {
189	        autoChecksum: frameOptions.autoChecksum,
190	        bigEndian: frameOptions.bigEndian,
191	        includeLengthField: frameOptions.includeLengthField,
192	      } : undefined);
193	      const patched = applyBuildPostPatch(bytes, fields, patchOptions);
194	      allIssues.push(...patched.issues);
195	
196	      const patchErrors = allIssues.filter((i) => i.severity === 'error');
197	      if (patchErrors.length > 0) {
198	        const result = makeResult('build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0, undefined, allIssues);
199	        state.addResult(result);
200	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
201	        return result;
202	      }
203	
204	      bytes = patched.buffer;
205	      const bytesArr = [...bytes];
206	
207	      // 7. Resolve target
208	      const target = options.targetResolver.resolveTarget(request.targetId);
209	      if (!target) {
210	        const result = makeResult(
211	          'target-unavailable', requestRef, timestamp, bytesArr.length, 0,
212	          { kind: 'target-not-found', message: `Target not found: ${request.targetId}` },
213	        );
214	        state.addResult(result);
215	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
216	        return result;
217	      }
218	
219	      if (!target.available) {
220	        const result = makeResult(
221	          'target-unavailable', requestRef, timestamp, bytesArr.length, 0,
222	          { kind: 'target-not-available', message: `Target not available: ${request.targetId}` },
223	        );
224	        state.addResult(result);
225	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
226	        return result;
227	      }
228	
229	      // 8. Transport write
230	      let writeOutcome: SendTransportWriteOutcome;
231	      try {
232	        writeOutcome = await options.transportWriter.writeBytes(target.connectionId, bytesArr);
233	      } catch (err) {
234	        const result = makeResult(
235	          'transport-error', requestRef, timestamp, bytesArr.length, 0,
236	          { kind: 'write-exception', message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown write error' },
237	          allIssues,
238	        );
239	        state.addResult(result);
240	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
241	        return result;
242	      }
243	
244	      if (!writeOutcome.ok) {
245	        const result = makeResult(
246	          writeOutcome.error?.kind === 'timeout' ? 'timeout' : 'transport-error',
247	          requestRef,
248	          timestamp,
249	          bytesArr.length,
250	          writeOutcome.bytesWritten,
251	          writeOutcome.error,
252	          allIssues,
253	        );
254	        state.addResult(result);
255	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
256	        return result;
257	      }
258	
259	      // 9. Success
260	      const result = makeResult(
261	        'sent',
262	        requestRef,
263	        timestamp,
264	        bytesArr.length,
265	        writeOutcome.bytesWritten,
266	        undefined,
267	        allIssues,
268	      );
269	      state.addResult(result);
270	      options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
271	      return result;
272	    },
273	
274	    resetStats() {
275	      return state.resetStats();
276	    },
277	  };
278	}
279	
```

```
1	import { shallowRef } from 'vue';
2	import type { TaskService } from '../services';
3	import type { ReadonlyTaskInstanceState } from '../core';
4	import { selectActiveInstances, selectTaskHistory } from '../selectors';
5	import { resolveDisplayStatus } from '../components/taskStatusMap';
6	import { calculateProgress } from '../core';
7	import type { TaskTableRow } from '../components/task-columns';
8	import type { HistoryTableRow } from '../components/history-columns';
9	
10	function toTaskRow(inst: ReadonlyTaskInstanceState): TaskTableRow {
11	  const displayStatus = resolveDisplayStatus(inst.lifecycle, inst.definitionRef);
12	  const progress = calculateProgress(inst);
13	  const total = progress.stepsTotal || 1;
14	  const pct = Math.round((progress.stepsCompleted / total) * 100);
15	  return {
16	    instanceId: inst.instanceId,
17	    name: inst.definitionRef.name,
18	    scheduleKind: inst.definitionRef.schedule.kind,
19	    lifecycle: inst.lifecycle,
20	    displayStatus,
21	    progressPercent: pct,
22	    progressLabel: `${progress.stepsCompleted}/${progress.stepsTotal}`,
23	    _original: inst,
24	  };
25	}
26	
27	function toHistoryRow(inst: ReadonlyTaskInstanceState): HistoryTableRow {
28	  const startedAt = inst.startedAt ? new Date(inst.startedAt).getTime() : 0;
29	  const endRef = inst.completedAt ?? inst.stoppedAt ?? inst.failedAt;
30	  const endTime = endRef ? new Date(endRef).getTime() : Date.now();
31	  const elapsedMs = startedAt > 0 ? Math.max(0, endTime - startedAt) : 0;
32	  const finishedAt = inst.completedAt ?? inst.stoppedAt ?? inst.failedAt ?? '';
33	  return {
34	    instanceId: inst.instanceId,
35	    name: inst.definitionRef.name,
36	    scheduleKind: inst.definitionRef.schedule.kind,
37	    lifecycle: inst.lifecycle,
38	    elapsedMs,
39	    finishedAt,
40	    _original: inst,
41	  };
42	}
43	
44	export function useTaskList(taskService: TaskService, mode: 'active' | 'history') {
45	  const rows = shallowRef<TaskTableRow[]>([]);
46	  const historyRows = shallowRef<HistoryTableRow[]>([]);
47	
48	  function refresh(): void {
49	    const snapshot = taskService.getSnapshot();
50	    if (mode === 'active') {
51	      const active = selectActiveInstances(snapshot);
52	      rows.value = active.map(toTaskRow);
53	    } else {
54	      const history = selectTaskHistory(snapshot);
55	      historyRows.value = history.map(toHistoryRow);
56	    }
57	  }
58	
59	  function selectAllRows(): readonly (TaskTableRow | HistoryTableRow)[] {
60	    return mode === 'active' ? rows.value : historyRows.value;
61	  }
62	
63	  return { rows, historyRows, refresh, selectAllRows };
64	}
65
```

```
1	import type { TaskInstanceState, TaskProgress, TaskStepResult } from './types';
2	import { resolveStopCondition } from './types';
3	
4	export function calculateProgress(instance: TaskInstanceState): TaskProgress {
5	  const stepsPerIteration = instance.definitionRef.steps.length;
6	  const { currentIteration, stepResults } = instance;
7	
8	  const currentIterResults = stepResults.filter((r) => r.iteration === currentIteration);
9	
10	  let stepsCompleted = 0;
11	  let stepsFailed = 0;
12	  let stepsSkipped = 0;
13	
14	  for (const result of currentIterResults) {
15	    if (result.appliedPolicy === 'skip-step') {
16	      stepsSkipped++;
17	    } else if (isStepResultFailed(result)) {
18	      stepsFailed++;
19	    } else {
20	      stepsCompleted++;
21	    }
22	  }
23	
24	  const iterationsCompleted = countCompletedIterations(instance, stepsPerIteration);
25	  const iterationsTotal = resolveIterationsTotal(instance);
26	  const elapsedMs = calculateElapsedMs(instance);
27	
28	  const estimatedRemainingMs = estimateRemainingMs(instance, iterationsCompleted, iterationsTotal, elapsedMs);
29	  const lastStepResult = stepResults.length > 0 ? stepResults[stepResults.length - 1] : null;
30	
31	  return {
32	    stepsTotal: stepsPerIteration,
33	    stepsCompleted,
34	    stepsFailed,
35	    stepsSkipped,
36	    iterationsCompleted,
37	    iterationsTotal,
38	    elapsedMs,
39	    estimatedRemainingMs,
40	    lastStepResult,
41	  };
42	}
43	
44	export function isStepResultFailed(result: TaskStepResult): boolean {
45	  switch (result.kind) {
46	    case 'send':
47	      return result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent';
48	    case 'wait-condition':
49	      return !result.matched && result.timedOut;
50	    case 'delay':
51	      return !result.completed;
52	  }
53	}
54	
55	function countCompletedIterations(instance: TaskInstanceState, stepsPerIteration: number): number {
56	  const iterationIndices = new Set(instance.stepResults.map((r) => r.iteration));
57	  let count = 0;
58	  for (const iter of iterationIndices) {
59	    const iterResults = instance.stepResults.filter((r) => r.iteration === iter);
60	    if (iterResults.length >= stepsPerIteration) {
61	      count++;
62	    }
63	  }
64	  return count;
65	}
66	
67	function resolveIterationsTotal(instance: TaskInstanceState): number | null {
68	  const resolved = resolveStopCondition(instance.definitionRef);
69	  const { schedule } = instance.definitionRef;
70	
71	  if (resolved.maxIterations !== undefined) {
72	    return resolved.maxIterations;
73	  }
74	  if (schedule.kind === 'immediate') {
75	    return 1;
76	  }
77	  return null;
78	}
79	
80	function calculateElapsedMs(instance: TaskInstanceState): number {
81	  if (!instance.startedAt) return 0;
82	  const start = new Date(instance.startedAt).getTime();
83	  const endRef = instance.completedAt ?? instance.stoppedAt ?? instance.failedAt;
84	  const end = endRef ? new Date(endRef).getTime() : Date.now();
85	  return Math.max(0, end - start);
86	}
87	
88	function estimateRemainingMs(
89	  instance: TaskInstanceState,
90	  iterationsCompleted: number,
91	  iterationsTotal: number | null,
92	  elapsedMs: number,
93	): number | null {
94	  if (instance.definitionRef.schedule.kind !== 'timer' || iterationsTotal === null) {
95	    return null;
96	  }
97	  if (iterationsCompleted <= 0 || iterationsTotal <= iterationsCompleted) {
98	    return null;
99	  }
100	  const avgIterMs = elapsedMs / iterationsCompleted;
101	  return Math.round(avgIterMs * (iterationsTotal - iterationsCompleted));
102	}
103	
```

```
1	import { computed, type Ref } from 'vue';
2	import type { ReadonlyFrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';
3	import type { SendFieldValue } from '../core';
4	import {
5	  frameToBuildInput,
6	  resolveFieldValues,
7	  applyFactor,
8	  buildFrame,
9	  applyBuildPostPatch,
10	  evaluateFieldPreviewForUI,
11	} from '../core';
12	import { NOOP_VARIABLE_PROVIDER } from '../adapters';
13	
14	export interface FramePreviewResult {
15	  readonly hexPreview: string;
16	  readonly fieldPreviews: ReadonlyArray<{
17	    readonly fieldId: string;
18	    readonly fieldName: string;
19	    readonly value: SendFieldValue;
20	  }>;
21	  readonly issues: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly severity: string; readonly message: string }>;
22	}
23	
24	export function useFramePreview(
25	  frame: Ref<ReadonlyFrameAsset | null>,
26	  userFieldValues: Ref<Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>>,
27	) {
28	  const fullPreview = computed<FramePreviewResult>(() => {
29	    const f = frame.value;
30	    if (!f) {
31	      return { hexPreview: '', fieldPreviews: [], issues: [] };
32	    }
33	
34	    try {
35	      const { fields, totalByteLength } = frameToBuildInput(f);
36	      const provider = NOOP_VARIABLE_PROVIDER;
37	
38	      // Resolve all field values
39	      const resolved = resolveFieldValues(fields, userFieldValues.value, provider);
40	      const factored = applyFactor(fields, resolved.values);
41	      const allIssues = [...resolved.issues, ...factored.issues];
42	
43	      // Build frame bytes
44	      const buildOutput = buildFrame({
45	        fields,
46	        totalByteLength,
47	        fieldValues: factored.values,
48	      });
49	      allIssues.push(...buildOutput.issues);
50	
51	      // Apply post-patch (checksum, length)
52	      const patched = applyBuildPostPatch(buildOutput.bytes, fields, {
53	        autoChecksum: f.options?.autoChecksum,
54	        bigEndian: f.options?.bigEndian,
55	        includeLengthField: f.options?.includeLengthField,
56	      });
57	      allIssues.push(...patched.issues);
58	
59	      // Format hex preview: byte-space-separated
60	      const hexBytes = [...patched.buffer]
61	        .map((b) => b.toString(16).toUpperCase().padStart(2, '0'));
62	      const hexPreview = hexBytes.join(' ');
63	
64	      // Per-field previews
65	      const fieldPreviews = f.fields.map((field) => {
66	        const preview = evaluateFieldPreviewForUI(
67	          f,
68	          field.id,
69	          userFieldValues.value,
70	        NOOP_VARIABLE_PROVIDER,
71	        );
72	        return {
73	          fieldId: field.id,
74	          fieldName: field.name,
75	          value: preview.value,
76	        };
77	      });
78	
79	      return {
80	        hexPreview,
81	        fieldPreviews,
82	        issues: allIssues.map((i) => ({ severity: i.severity, message: i.message })),
83	      };
84	    } catch (err) {
85	      console.warn('frame preview failed', err);
86	      return { hexPreview: '', fieldPreviews: [], issues: [] };
87	    }
88	  });
89	
90	  return { fullPreview };
91	}
92	
```

```json
  "old_string": "function refreshLists(): void {\n  const snapshot = taskService.getSnapshot();\n  const active = snapshot.instances.filter(\n    (i) => !isTerminal(i.lifecycle),\n  );\n  activeRows.value = active.map(toActiveRow);\n\n  const terminated = snapshot.instances.filter((i) => isTerminal(i.lifecycle));\n  const historyIds = new Set(snapshot.history.map((i) => i.instanceId));\n  const merged = [...snapshot.history, ...terminated.filter((i) => !historyIds.has(i.instanceId))];\n  historyRows.value = merged.map(toHistoryRow);\n}\n\nfunction toActiveRow(inst: ReadonlyTaskInstanceState): TaskTableRow {\n  const displayStatus = resolveDisplayStatus(inst.lifecycle, inst.definitionRef);\n  const progress = inst.startedAt\n    ? (() => {\n        const stepsPerIteration = inst.definitionRef.steps.length;\n        const currentIterResults = inst.stepResults.filter((r) => r.iteration === inst.currentIteration);\n        let stepsCompleted = 0;\n        for (const r of currentIterResults) {\n          if (r.appliedPolicy !== 'skip-step') stepsCompleted++;\n        }\n        return { completed: stepsCompleted, total: stepsPerIteration };\n      })()\n    : { completed: 0, total: inst.definitionRef.steps.length };\n  const pct = progress.total > 0 ? Math.round((progress.completed / progress.total) * 100) : 0;\n  return {\n    instanceId: inst.instanceId,\n    name: inst.definitionRef.name,\n    scheduleKind: inst.definitionRef.schedule.kind,\n    lifecycle: inst.lifecycle,\n    displayStatus,\n    progressPercent: pct,\n    progressLabel: `${progress.completed}/${progress.total}`,\n    _original: inst,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction toHistoryRow(inst: ReadonlyTaskInstanceState): HistoryTableRow {\n  const startedAt = inst.startedAt ? new Date(inst.startedAt).getTime() : 0;\n  const endRef = inst.completedAt ?? inst.stoppedAt ?? inst.failedAt;\n  const endTime = endRef ? new Date(endRef).getTime() : Date.now();\n  const elapsedMs = startedAt > 0 ? Math.max(0, endTime - startedAt) : 0;\n  return {\n    instanceId: inst.instanceId,\n    name: inst.definitionRef.name,\n    scheduleKind: inst.definitionRef.schedule.kind,\n    lifecycle: inst.lifecycle,\n    elapsedMs,\n    finishedAt: inst.completedAt ?? inst.stoppedAt ?? inst.failedAt ?? '',\n    _original: inst,\n  };\n}",
  "new_string": "function refreshLists(): void {\n  const snapshot = taskService.getSnapshot();\n  const active = snapshot.instances.filter(\n    (i) => !isTerminal(i.lifecycle),\n  );\n  activeRows.value = active.map(toTaskRow);\n\n  const terminated = snapshot.instances.filter((i) => isTerminal(i.lifecycle));\n  const historyIds = new Set(snapshot.history.map((i) => i.instanceId));\n  const merged = [...snapshot.history, ...terminated.filter((i) => !historyIds.has(i.instanceId))];\n  historyRows.value = merged.map(toHistoryRow);\n}"
}
```

```json
  "content": "import { cloneFrameAsset, type FrameAssetSummary, type FrameService } from '../';\nimport type { Notify } from '@/shared/composables';\n\nexport function useToggleFavorite(\n  frameService: FrameService,\n  notify: Notify,\n  onSuccess?: () => void,\n) {\n  function toggleFavorite(frame: FrameAssetSummary): void {\n    const full = frameService.getFrame(frame.id);\n    if (!full) return;\n    const cloned = cloneFrameAsset(full);\n    cloned.isFavorite = !cloned.isFavorite;\n    const result = frameService.upsertFrame(cloned);\n    if (result.ok) {\n      onSuccess?.();\n    } else {\n      notify.error('操作失败');\n    }\n  }\n\n  return { toggleFavorite };\n}\n"
}
```

```
1	import { cloneFrameAsset, type FrameAssetSummary, type FrameService } from '../';
2	import type { Notify } from '@/shared/composables';
3	
4	export function useToggleFavorite(
5	  frameService: FrameService,
6	  notify: Notify,
7	  onSuccess?: () => void,
8	) {
9	  function toggleFavorite(frame: FrameAssetSummary): void {
10	    const full = frameService.getFrame(frame.id);
11	    if (!full) return;
12	    const cloned = cloneFrameAsset(full);
13	    cloned.isFavorite = !cloned.isFavorite;
14	    const result = frameService.upsertFrame(cloned);
15	    if (result.ok) {
16	      onSuccess?.();
17	    } else {
18	      notify.error('操作失败');
19	    }
20	  }
21	
22	  return { toggleFavorite };
23	}
24
```

```
1	import type { ProtocolAdapter, ParsedCommand } from '../core';
2	import type {
3	  ScoeGlobalConfig,
4	  ScoeCommandConfig,
5	  ChecksumConfig,
6	  ScoeCommandParam,
7	} from '../core';
8	import type { CommandIngressStateReader } from '../core';
9	import type { TransportEventSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';
10	import type { ConsumerResult } from '@/runtime/consumer-chain';
11	
12	export interface ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions {
13	  readonly globalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig;
14	  readonly commandConfigs: readonly ScoeCommandConfig[];
15	  readonly stateReader: CommandIngressStateReader;
16	  readonly onCommand?: (parsed: ParsedCommand) => Promise<void>;
17	  readonly onConsume?: (bytes: readonly number[]) => void;
18	}
19	
20	export function createScoeProtocolAdapter(
21	  options: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions,
22	): ProtocolAdapter {
23	  const { globalConfig, stateReader, onCommand, onConsume } = options;
24	  // Base configs used before LOAD; after LOAD, state.activeCommandConfigs takes over
25	  const baseCommandConfigs = [...options.commandConfigs];
26	
27	  function getCommandConfigs(): readonly ScoeCommandConfig[] {
28	    const active = stateReader.getSnapshot().activeCommandConfigs;
29	    if (active.length === 0) return baseCommandConfigs;
30	    // Merge: satellite-specific configs + base built-in commands (LOAD/UNLOAD/HEALTH/LINK)
31	    const builtInFunctions = new Set(['load_satellite_id', 'unload_satellite_id', 'health_check', 'link_check']);
32	    const satelliteFunctions = new Set(active.map((c) => c.function));
33	    const baseBuiltIns = baseCommandConfigs.filter((c) => builtInFunctions.has(c.function) && !satelliteFunctions.has(c.function));
34	    return [...active, ...baseBuiltIns];
35	  }
36	
37	  function canHandle(event: TransportEventSnapshot): boolean {
38	    if (event.kind !== 'data' || event.bytes === undefined) return false;
39	
40	    const bytes = event.bytes;
41	    const fcOffset = globalConfig.functionCodeOffset;
42	
43	    if (bytes.length < fcOffset + 4) return false;
44	
45	    const identifierByte = bytes[fcOffset];
46	    const expectedIdentifier = hexToByte(globalConfig.scoeIdentifier);
47	    if (expectedIdentifier === null || identifierByte !== expectedIdentifier) return false;
48	
49	    // Fixed 0xAA 0xAA at offset +2 and +3
50	    if (bytes[fcOffset + 2] !== 0xaa || bytes[fcOffset + 3] !== 0xaa) return false;
51	
52	    // Two-phase state machine
53	    if (!stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded) {
54	      // Phase 1: only match LOAD_SATELLITE_ID (command code 01)
55	      const commandCode = byteToHex(bytes[fcOffset + 1]!);
56	      if (commandCode !== '01') return false;
57	    }
58	
59	    return true;
60	  }
61	
62	  function parse(event: TransportEventSnapshot): ParsedCommand {
63	    if (event.bytes === undefined) throw new Error('Event has no bytes');
64	
65	    const bytes = event.bytes;
66	    const fcOffset = globalConfig.functionCodeOffset;
67	
68	    const codeByte = bytes[fcOffset + 1];
69	    if (codeByte === undefined) throw new Error('Command code byte missing');
70	
71	    const commandCode = byteToHex(codeByte);
72	    const commandConfig = getCommandConfigs().find((c) => c.code === commandCode);
73	    if (!commandConfig) {
74	      throw new Error(`Unknown SCOE command code: ${commandCode}`);
75	    }
76	
77	    validateChecksums(bytes, commandConfig.checksums);
78	
79	    const resolvedParams = extractAndResolveParams(bytes, commandConfig.params ?? []);
80	
81	    return {
82	      commandId: `scoe-cmd-${event.id}`,
83	      commandCode,
84	      commandFunction: commandConfig.function,
85	      rawBytes: [...bytes],
86	      resolvedParams,
87	      commandConfig,
88	      connectionId: event.connectionId,
89	      occurredAt: event.occurredAt,
90	    };
91	  }
92	
93	  return {
94	    id: 'scoe-protocol',
95	    protocolId: 'scoe-tcp',
96	
97	    async consume(events: readonly TransportEventSnapshot[]): Promise<ConsumerResult> {
98	      const consumed: TransportEventSnapshot[] = [];
99	      const remaining: TransportEventSnapshot[] = [];
100	
101	      for (const event of events) {
102	        if (canHandle(event)) {
103	          try {
104	            const parsed = parse(event);
105	            if (onCommand) {
106	              await onCommand(parsed);
107	            }
108	          } catch (err) {
109	            console.warn(`SCOE parse error: ${(err as Error).message}`);
110	          }
111	          consumed.push(event);
112	          if (onConsume && event.bytes) onConsume(event.bytes);
113	        } else {
114	          remaining.push(event);
115	        }
116	      }
117	
118	      return { consumed, remaining };
119	    },
120	  };
121	}
122	
123	// --- Byte-level helpers (exported for testing) ---
124	
125	export function byteToHex(byte: number): string {
126	  return byte.toString(16).padStart(2, '0').toUpperCase();
127	}
128	
129	export function hexToByte(hex: string): number | null {
130	  const cleaned = hex.replace(/^0x/i, '');
131	  const value = parseInt(cleaned, 16);
132	  return Number.isNaN(value) ? null : value;
133	}
134	
135	export function validateChecksums(
136	  bytes: readonly number[],
137	  checksums: readonly ChecksumConfig[],
138	): void {
139	  for (const cs of checksums) {
140	    if (!cs.enabled) continue;
141	
142	    let sum = 0;
143	    for (let i = cs.offset; i < cs.offset + cs.length; i++) {
144	      sum += bytes[i] ?? 0;
145	    }
146	    const expected = sum % 256;
147	    const actual = bytes[cs.checksumOffset] ?? 0;
148	
149	    if (expected !== actual) {
150	      throw new Error(
151	        `Checksum failed at offset ${cs.checksumOffset}: expected ${expected}, got ${actual}`,
152	      );
153	    }
154	  }
155	}
156	
157	export function extractAndResolveParams(
158	  bytes: readonly number[],
159	  params: readonly ScoeCommandParam[],
160	): Record<string, string> {
161	  const result: Record<string, string> = {};
162	
163	  for (const param of params) {
164	    const slice = bytes.slice(param.offset, param.offset + param.length);
165	    const hex = bytesToUpperCaseHex(slice);
166	    const normalized = normalizeHex(hex, param.length * 2);
167	
168	    const matched = param.options.find(
169	      (opt) => normalizeHex(opt.receiveCode, param.length * 2) === normalized,
170	    );
171	
172	    result[param.id] = matched?.value ?? hex;
173	  }
174	
175	  return result;
176	}
177	
178	function bytesToUpperCaseHex(bytes: readonly number[]): string {
179	  return Array.from(bytes)
180	    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0').toUpperCase())
181	    .join('');
182	}
183	
184	function normalizeHex(hex: string, targetLength: number): string {
185	  const cleaned = hex.replace(/^0x/i, '').toUpperCase();
186	  return cleaned.padStart(targetLength, '0');
187	}
188	
```

```
157	        if (inst?.lifecycle === 'completed') {
158	          try {
159	            await sendAckFrame(command, ctx);
160	          } catch {
161	            // Ack frame send failure — don't crash, just count as error
162	          }
163	          stateWriter.incrementSuccessCount();
164	          logRecorder.complete(logEntryId, {
165	            result: 'success',
166	            durationMs: inst.completedAt && inst.startedAt
167	              ? new Date(inst.completedAt).getTime() - new Date(inst.startedAt).getTime()
168	              : undefined,
169	          });
```

> AGENT

该组件在一个 `<template v-else-if="!isTerminal(selectedInstance.lifecycle)">` 内部，所以 `selectedInstance` 保证是非空的。但严格来说，模板应该使用 `selectedInstance?.instanceId` 或者更好的做法——使用处理器封装。由于 Vue 的模板已经保护了这一点（它在 `<template v-else-if>` 内部），`!` 实际上是安全的。不过，为了整洁，还是添加空值守卫吧。

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: call_dbe8b2f5835141118d7d5292
```json

  "command": "sed -i 's/onPause(selectedInstance!\\.instanceId)/onPause(selectedInstance?.instanceId ?? '')/g; s/onResume(selectedInstance!\\.instanceId)/onResume(selectedInstance?.instanceId ?? '')/g; s/onStop(selectedInstance!\\.instanceId)/onStop(selectedInstance?.instanceId ?? '')/g' /mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/pages/TaskManagePage.vue",
  "description": "Replace non-null assertions with null guards"
}
```
